To enhance representation quality, we developed a novel 3D depth-aware pre-training strategy anchored by dense auxiliary supervision from both automated anatomical masks and human-annotated abnormalities. Under linear probe evaluation with Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) for multi-abnormality detection, the frozen backbone of this dual-supervised model (DALE-CT-2S) achieves a Macro AUROC of 0.833. This performance demonstrates near-parity with state-of-the-art 3D vision-language models, achieved entirely from scratch with significantly less data and no textual supervision. To ensure reproducibility, all training code, evaluation scripts, and model weights have been made publicly available.

</details>


### [38] [The Last Visible Pixel: Probing Fine-Scale Perception in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07861)
*Lujun Li,Lama Sleem,Niccolo Gentile,Yangjie Xu,Yewei Song,Wenbo Wu,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了FineSightBench基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在精细视觉感知方面的极限，揭示了感知能力在约12像素时达到饱和，而精细推理能力即使在更大尺度下仍然有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管当前视觉语言模型在多模态理解和推理方面表现出色，但其精细视觉感知能力尚未得到充分探索。研究关注模型能够可靠感知的最小视觉模式，以评估其在像素级别识别和精细推理方面的实际能力限制。

Method: 引入了FineSightBench基准测试，通过系统性地分离感知任务（像素级别的字母、形状、对象识别）和推理任务（空间推理、计数、小目标排序）来探究模型极限。在4-48像素的受控尺度范围内进行实验，并对最先进模型进行详细的故障模式分析。

Result: 实验揭示了明显的分离现象：感知能力在约12像素时达到饱和，而推理能力即使在更大尺度下仍然有限。模型在精细尺度推理方面存在持续性的计数和序列排序错误。最先进模型在精细视觉推理方面存在基本缺陷。

Conclusion: 视觉语言模型在精细尺度视觉推理方面存在基本缺陷，需要进行更严格的评估。FineSightBench为系统评估模型的视觉感知极限提供了一个有价值的基准，并揭示了当前模型在精细视觉任务上的重要局限性。

Abstract: Recent vision-language models (VLMs) excel at multimodal understanding and reasoning, yet their fine-grained visual perception remains underexplored. A natural extension of ``How many r are there in Strawberry?'' asks: how small a visual pattern can a VLM reliably perceive? As such, we introduce FineSightBench, a new benchmark that systematically probes this limit by separating perception tasks (pixel-level recognition of letters, shapes, objects) from reasoning tasks (spatial reasoning, counting, ordering over small targets) across controlled scales of 4--48px. Through comprehensive experiments and detailed failure mode analysis on state-of-the-art models, we reveal a sharp dissociation: perception saturates around 12px, while reasoning remains limited even at larger scales, with persistent numeracy and sequence errors. These findings expose fundamental deficiencies in VLMs' fine-scale visual reasoning that demand more rigorous evaluation.

</details>


### [39] [VisualFLIP: Do Predictions Depend on Task-Critical Visual Evidence in Multimodal Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07872)
*Didi Zhu,Changrui Chen,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisualFLIP是一个用于评估多模态大语言模型视觉推理能力的配对基准，包含1,374个图像对，通过最小化改变任务关键视觉证据来强制正确答案翻转，使用配对准确率和崩溃率来评估模型对视觉证据的依赖程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在视觉推理任务中，即使给出正确答案，其预测也可能不受任务关键视觉证据支持，准确性本身不足以全面评估模型的视觉基础能力，需要更精细的评估方法来检测模型是否真正依赖视觉证据进行推理。

Method: 创建VisualFLIP基准，包含1,374个图像组成的相同问题扰动对，覆盖数量、属性、空间和逻辑任务。每个配对保持问题不变，但对任务关键视觉证据进行最小化修改，使正确答案确定性地翻转。使用配对准确率（要求正确回答配对的两边）和崩溃率（衡量模型在至少正确回答一边时对两张图像给出相同非空答案的频率）来评估24个MLLMs。

Result: 配对正确性和证据依赖性相关但不同：能力强的模型在任务关键视觉变化后仍可能无法更新答案，并且在顺序设置中，当编辑图像出现在先前答案之后时，某些模型的崩溃问题更加严重。

Conclusion: 仅凭准确性不足以评估多模态大语言模型的视觉基础能力，需要更精细的评估方法如VisualFLIP来检测模型是否真正依赖视觉证据。配对准确率和崩溃率揭示了模型在视觉变化下的推理弱点，为改进模型提供了重要见解。

Abstract: When a multimodal large language model answers a visual reasoning question correctly, is the prediction actually supported by the task-critical visual evidence? Correct answers can coexist with flawed reasoning, making accuracy alone an incomplete test of grounding. We introduce VisualFLIP, a paired benchmark with 1,374 images arranged as same-question perturbation pairs across cardinality, attribute, spatial, and logic tasks. Each pair keeps the question fixed but minimally changes the evidence so the gold answer deterministically flips. We evaluate 24 MLLMs with pair accuracy, which requires solving both sides of a pair, and Collapse Rate (CR), which measures how often a model that solves at least one side repeats the same non-empty answer for both images. Together, these metrics show that paired correctness and evidence dependence are related but distinct: capable models can still fail to update after task-critical visual changes, and collapse becomes more severe for some models when the edited image follows an earlier answer in a sequential setting. Further details are available on our project page: https://didizhu-judy.github.io/VisualFLIP/

</details>


### [40] [The Cross-Architecture Substrate: A Domain-Transcendent, Calibration-Surviving Geometric Invariant of Modern Vision Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07882)
*Yousef Radwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究发现，13种现代视觉编码器在训练后，其内部表示的前16个主方向会收敛到相同的16维几何结构（跨架构基底），该基底在视觉域间具有强泛化性，可用于多种应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同视觉神经网络（用于分类、对比、重建或图像-文本匹配）理论上应有不同的内部表示，但研究发现它们实际上收敛到相同的底层结构，这揭示了视觉表示学习中的共性。

Method: 使用PCA分析13种现代视觉编码器的内部表示，通过中心核对齐（CKA）和Pang 2026校准验证跨架构基底的存在性，并在四个视觉域（自然照片、医学CT、卫星、显微镜）和八个域上进行泛化性测试。

Result: 发现跨架构基底在视觉域间具有强一致性（中位Procrustes-CKA 0.679/0.604），不是像素统计、Gabor特征或随机投影，在训练早期出现。开发了四种应用：无标签迁移性筛选器、四域检测器、冻结少样本探针和教师无关蒸馏辅助。

Conclusion: 视觉编码器在训练后收敛到共同的16维几何基底，该基底具有跨域泛化能力，可用于多种实际应用，但不跨模态、不帮助跨范式蒸馏，且不预测迁移质量。

Abstract: Different vision neural networks -- trained to classify, contrast, reconstruct, or match images to text -- should have correspondingly different internal representations. We report that they do not. After training, the top sixteen principal directions of variation inside thirteen modern vision encoders converge to the same sixteen-dimensional geometric object. We call this the cross-architecture substrate and study it with PCA, centred kernel alignment (CKA), and Pang 2026 calibration. The substrate transports across four visual domains (natural photographs, medical CT, satellite, microscopy) at median Procrustes-CKA 0.679, and across eight domains (adding sketches, depth, thermal infrared, astronomy) at 0.604, every pair >0.40. It survives Pang calibration globally (7.4x disc-vs-MAE separation, n=13,394) and locally (4.82-5.30, p<10^{-44}). It is not pixel statistics (0.263), not Gabor features (0.31), not a random projection (0.041), and emerges in the first 10% of training while accuracy keeps climbing. We deliver four applications: a label-free transferability filter beating LogME (3x faster, +0.15 Kendall-tau); a four-way domain detector (99.6% accuracy); a frozen low-shot probe (16 dims beat 768-dim DINOv2 by 3.78pp at N=50 labels per class); and a teacher-free distillation auxiliary matching trained-teacher KD on 33 pairs (7.56pp peak gain at 10% label fraction). The substrate does not cross modalities, does not help cross-paradigm distillation, and does not predict transfer quality (rho=0.08 against transfer accuracy).

</details>


### [41] [C3VD-DEFCOL: A Deformable Colonoscopy Dataset with Time-Resolved 3D Ground Truth and Realistic Appearance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07891)
*Ethan Luk,Mayank V. Golhar,Anthony Song,Raúl Iranzo,Víctor M. Batlle,Lalithkumar Seenivasan,José M. M. Montiel,Nicholas J. Durr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C3VD-DEFCOL是一个用于评估可变形结肠镜重建的数据集和框架，通过生成可控变形和逼真的内窥镜纹理，为算法开发提供几何和外观一致的3D真实标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据集无法同时提供真实体内外观和密集、时间分辨的3D真实标注，尤其是在非刚性变形条件下，限制了结肠镜3D重建算法的开发。

Method: 从C3VD/C3VDv2结肠网格和相机轨迹开始，生成结肠表面的可控变形（蠕动波和中心线运动），渲染每帧深度、表面法线、光流和相机姿态；然后使用渲染几何（主要是深度）训练LTX-2.3基础的模拟-真实转换模型，生成具有真实黏膜颜色、纹理、血管和镜面反射的RGB视频，同时保留底层3D场景结构。

Result: 创建了包含11个独特结肠网格几何、110个视频的数据集，涵盖不同相机轨迹、外观和参数化变形模式；评估显示生成的视频在外观真实性、几何一致性和时间一致性方面表现良好；实验表明随着变形严重程度增加，姿态估计误差增加，提供了现有体内数据集无法实现的受控压力测试。

Conclusion: C3VD-DEFCOL是一个可重复的定量评估平台，用于测试可变形3D重建算法，旨在减少合成数据集与体内结肠镜之间的领域差距，促进结肠镜3D重建算法的开发。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction could improve colonoscopy by estimating mucosal coverage and alerting clinicians to missed regions during screening. However, algorithm development is limited as no current datasets provide both a realistic in vivo appearance and dense, time-resolved 3D ground truth, especially under non-rigid deformation. We present C3VD-DEFCOL, a framework and dataset for evaluating deformable colonoscopy reconstruction with paired geometry and realistic texture. Starting from C3VD/C3VDv2 colon meshes and camera trajectories, we generate controlled deformations of the colon surface, including peristaltic waves and centerline motion, and render per-frame depth, surface normals, optical flow, camera poses, and time-stamped 3D meshes. We then use the rendered geometry, primarily depth, to condition an LTX-2.3-based sim-to-real translation model that produces RGB clips with in vivo-like mucosal color, texture, vasculature, and specular appearance while preserving the underlying 3D scene structure. The resulting dataset contains 110 videos from 11 unique colon mesh geometries, with varying camera trajectories, appearances, and parameterized deformation regimes, including three peristaltic severity levels that serve as controlled evaluation axes. We evaluate the generated videos using appearance realism, geometric consistency, and temporal consistency metrics, and use the paired ground truth to benchmark the downstream task of pose estimation in deformable 3D reconstruction. Our experiments show how pose estimation error increases with increasing deformation severity, providing a controlled stress test that is not possible with existing in vivo datasets. Overall, C3VD-DEFCOL is designed as a reproducible, quantitative evaluation platform for testing deformable 3D reconstruction algorithms, with the goal of reducing the domain gap between synthetic datasets and in vivo colonoscopy.

</details>


### [42] [TBD-VLA: Temporal Block Diffusion Vision Language Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07895)
*Sung-Wook Lee,Xuhui Kang,Yen-Ling Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TBD-VLA是一个基于离散令牌的视觉-语言-动作框架，通过块扩散技术实现时间动作生成，在保持时间连贯性的同时提高推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散VLA模型将动作生成视为离散动作空间上的下一个令牌预测，存在推理延迟高且忽略动作轨迹时间结构的问题。并行解码方法虽然提高了效率，但缺乏对令牌依赖关系的显式建模机制。

Method: 将动作序列划分为时间块，在每个块内执行掩码离散扩散，同时在块之间保持自回归生成。这种设计统一了时间自回归和平行动作解码，实现了时间连贯性和推理速度的提升。

Result: TBD-VLA在模拟和真实世界操作任务中显著优于先前的VLA方法，通过时间修复实现了动作块的异步执行（如实时分块）。

Conclusion: TBD-VLA提供了一个可扩展的路径，用于构建快速、具有时间感知的离散VLA模型，统一了时间建模和高效推理。

Abstract: Discrete Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically formulate action generation as next-token prediction over discretized action spaces, conditioning each token autoregressively on prior context. While effective, this paradigm incurs high inference latency and largely ignores the temporal structure inherent in action trajectories. Recent efforts introduce parallel decoding to improve efficiency, enabling faster inference, but lack explicit mechanisms for modeling token dependencies. We introduce TBD-VLA, a discrete token-based VLA framework that incorporates block diffusion to enable temporal action generation. We partition action sequences into temporal blocks and perform masked discrete diffusion within each block, while maintaining autoregressive generation across blocks. This design unifies temporal autoregression and parallel action decoding, achieving both strong temporal coherence and improved inference speed. In addition, the explicit temporal modeling enables asynchronous execution of action chunks (e.g., Real-Time Chunking) via temporal in-painting. TBD-VLA significantly outperforms prior VLA approaches in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, offering a scalable path toward fast, temporally aware, discrete VLA models. Project webpage: https://tbd-vla.github.io/

</details>


### [43] [3D Oral Modelling with Improved Vertex Distribution Using Matching-Based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07907)
*Jihun Cho,Soo-Yeon Jeong,Eun-Jeong Bae,Sun-Young Ihm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了改进的损失函数来解决先前3D口腔内重建模型中的顶点聚类问题，通过匈牙利匹配筛选和排斥损失使预测顶点分布更均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前基于深度学习的3D口腔内重建模型虽然达到77.49%的准确率，但预测顶点倾向于集中在真实点云的高密度区域，导致其他区域覆盖率不足，存在顶点聚类问题。

Method: 提出改进的损失函数，引入匈牙利匹配与筛选机制以及排斥损失，旨在强制预测顶点在重建模型表面更均匀分布，而不是集中在局部区域。

Result: 改进模型准确率为68.02%，数值上低于先前模型的77.49%。但顶点聚类问题得到显著缓解，预测顶点在整个重建表面分布更均匀。

Conclusion: 尽管准确率数值下降，但改进的损失函数有效解决了顶点分布不均问题，提升了重建模型的均匀性和覆盖完整性，这对3D重建的实用性和质量有重要意义。

Abstract: In our previous work, a deep learning-based framework for 3D intraoral reconstruction was proposed. The model directly predicts explicit 3D point cloud coordinates from ten fixed-angle intraoral images, employing MobileNetV2 and Multi-head Attention for multi-view feature fusion, with a combined L1 Loss and Chamfer Distance as the loss function. Although the model achieved an accuracy of 77.49%, predicted vertices tended to concentrate in high-density regions of the ground truth, leaving other regions largely uncovered.
  In this paper, an improved loss function is proposed to address this limitation. Hungarian matching with filtering and Repulsion Loss are introduced to enforce more uniform vertex distribution across the reconstructed model. The proposed model achieves an accuracy of 68.02%, which is numerically lower than the previous model. However, the vertex clustering issue observed in the prior work is substantially alleviated, with predicted vertices distributed more evenly across the entire reconstructed surface.

</details>


### [44] [Decoupling Semantics and Logic: A Training-Free Coarse-to-Fine Pipeline for Video Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07924)
*Jiaxin Dai,Zehang Wei,Jiamin Yan,Xiang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一个无需训练的两阶段级联视频RAG管道，解决跨语言长视频理解、严格角色遵循和零幻觉时间定位的挑战，通过语义检索与认知逻辑推理的解耦，在检索评估中展现出卓越精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨语言长视频理解的三个关键挑战：1) 严格角色(persona)遵循要求；2) 零幻觉时间定位(zero-hallucination temporal grounding)；3) 复杂多模态环境下的精确信息检索。

Method: 采用训练免费的两阶段级联视频RAG管道：第一阶段使用高召回语义预取模块，仅基于高保真视觉摘要和全局文本描述进行密集检索，隔离噪声模态(OCR、ASR)；第二阶段使用由商业LLM驱动的自适应迭代推理过滤代理(A.I.R.)，重新纳入完整多模态上下文进行细粒度认知重排，强制执行与用户角色的严格逻辑对齐；最后通过提示雕刻机制生成严格格式化的JSON响应和精确块级引用。

Result: 在RAG赛道评估中，该资源感知方法在信息检索和角色条件生成方面都显示出卓越的精度。

Conclusion: 提出的两阶段视频RAG管道通过战略性地将语义检索与认知逻辑推理解耦，成功解决了跨语言长视频理解中的关键挑战，验证了模态感知分工和训练免费方法的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents our system description for the 2nd Workshop on Multimodal Augmented Generation via MultimodAl Retrieval (MAGMaR). Addressing the critical challenges of cross-lingual long-video comprehension, strict persona adherence, and zero-hallucination temporal grounding, we propose a fully training-free, two-stage cascaded Video RAG pipeline. Our architecture strategically decouples semantic retrieval from cognitive logical reasoning through a modality-aware division of labor. In the first stage, a high-recall semantic pre-fetching module employs dense retrieval using only high-fidelity visual summaries and global text descriptions, explicitly isolating noisy modalities (e.g., OCR and ASR) to maintain a pristine vector space. In the second stage, an Adaptive, Iterative, and Reasoning-based (A.I.R.) filtering agent, powered by a commercial Large Language Model (LLM), performs fine-grained cognitive reranking. The agent re-incorporates full multimodal contexts to enforce strict logical alignment with user personas, effectively pruning semantically similar but logically irrelevant candidates. Finally, a Prompt Sculpting mechanism constrains the generator to synthesize the distilled subset into strictly formatted JSON responses with exact chunk-level citations. Evaluated on the RAG track, our resource-aware approach shows exceptional precision in both information retrieval and persona-conditioned generation.

</details>


### [45] [LEGS: Laplacian-Enhanced Gaussian Splatting with a Nonlinear Weighted Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07932)
*Yongfei Guo,Qizhou Huo,Xuan Sun,Yuanhao Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LEGS：一种基于拉普拉斯增强的非线性加权高斯泼溅方法，通过二阶结构引导和非线性权重映射提升3D场景重建质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的光度损失对所有区域采用相同处理，限制了尖锐轮廓和精细细节的恢复。现有的边缘引导高斯泼溅(EGGS)主要依赖一阶梯度响应和线性加权，存在改进空间。

Method: 提出LEGS方法：1) 用二阶拉普拉斯结构引导替代一阶梯度引导；2) 通过非线性响应-权重函数将归一化拉普拉斯响应映射为像素级权重；3) 保持原始3DGS渲染管道不变，仅改进损失函数设计。

Result: 在Tanks&Temples和Mip-NeRF360数据集上的实验表明：LEGS比3DGS提高PSNR达1.68dB，比EGGS提高达0.52dB。将该策略集成到FastGS和FasterGS中可进一步提升PSNR达1.69dB。

Conclusion: LEGS作为一种通用的损失级扩展，有效增强了结构感知的高斯优化，在保持实时渲染能力的同时显著提升重建质量，在AR/VR、沉浸式可视化和实时3D内容生成中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become an efficient explicit representation for radiance field reconstruction and real-time novel view synthesis. However, its standard photometric loss treats flat and structure-rich regions similarly, which may limit the recovery of sharp contours and fine details. Edge-Guided Gaussian Splatting (EGGS) improves structure awareness through edge-guided weighting, but mainly relies on first-order gradient responses and linear weighting. In this paper, we propose LEGS, a Laplacian-Enhanced Gaussian Splatting method with a nonlinearly weighted loss. LEGS replaces first-order gradient guidance with second-order Laplacian structural guidance and maps the normalized Laplacian response into pixel-wise weights through nonlinear response-to-weight functions. The proposed loss improves structure-aware Gaussian optimization while keeping the original 3DGS rendering pipeline unchanged. Experiments on the full Tanks\&Temples and Mip-NeRF360 datasets show that LEGS improves peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) by up to 1.68 dB over 3DGS and up to 0.52 dB over EGGS. Incorporating the proposed second-order nonlinear weighting strategy into FastGS and FasterGS further improves PSNR by up to 1.69 dB, demonstrating its effectiveness as a general loss-level extension for Gaussian Splatting pipelines with potential applications in AR/VR, immersive visualization, and real-time 3D content generation.

</details>


### [46] [REACT 2026: The Fourth Multiple Appropriate Facial Reaction Generation Challenge: Personalised MAFRG and Appropriate EEG Reaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07935)
*Siyang Song,Micol Spitale,Zijian Wu,Xiangyu Kong,Cheng Luo,Cristina Palmero,German Barquero,Sergio Escalera,Michel Valstar,Mohamed Daoudi,Fabien Ringeval,Andrew Howes,Elisabeth Andre,Hatice Gunes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REACT 2026挑战赛旨在开发能够生成个性化、多样化、真实且同步的面部反应模型，利用MARS数据集并新增个性特征和脑电信号，提出四个子挑战任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在二元互动中，面对不同的说话者行为，需要多种合适的面部反应。当前虽然已有MAFRG（多重合适面部反应生成）的深度学习模型，但缺乏结合个性特征和神经生理信号的一对多个性化面部反应生成方法。

Method: 提供MARS数据集并新增Big-Five个性标签和EEG脑电记录，设计四个子挑战：离线通用MAFRG、离线个性化MAFRG、在线通用MAFRG、在线个性化MAFRG，并建立相应的基准模型。

Result: 提出了REACT 2026挑战赛框架，发布了挑战指南和四个子任务的基准模型，公开在GitHub仓库中，为研究人员提供了评估个性化面部反应生成模型的标准化平台。

Conclusion: REACT 2026挑战赛推动了个性化面部反应生成研究，通过结合行为、情感和神经生理信号，探索了当前二元互动建模中尚未充分研究的一对多个性化生成设置。

Abstract: In dyadic interactions, various human facial reactions could be appropriate for responding to each human speaker behaviour. Following the successful organisation of the REACT 2023, 2024 and 2025 challenge series, a body of generative deep learning (DL) models have been developed for the problem of multiple appropriate facial reaction generation (MAFRG). This year, we propose the REACT 2026 challenge encouraging the development and benchmarking of Machine Learning (ML) models that can generate multiple personalised, appropriate, diverse, realistic and synchronised human-style facial reactions expressed by a specific human listener for responding to each given speaker behaviour. As a key of the challenge, we continuously provide challenge participants with MARS dataset introduced by REACT 2025 but additionally provide individual-level Big-Five personality labels and EEG recordings. This introduces a new one-to-many personalised facial reaction generation setting combining human expressive behavioural, affective and neurophysiological signals, which remains largely unexplored in current dyadic interaction modelling. This paper also presents the challenge guidelines and new baselines on the four proposed sub-challenges: Offline generic and personalised MAFRG as well as Online generic and personalised MAFRG, respectively, which are publicly available at https://github.com/reactmultimodalchallenge/baseline_react2026.

</details>


### [47] [DAL-PCQA: Enabling Distortion-Level and Language-Driven Reasoning for Point Cloud Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07938)
*Swarna Chakraborty,Gabriel De Castro Araújo,Syeda Tasmi Faria,Marcelo M. Carvalho,Mylene C. Q. Farias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAL-PCQA是一个失真感知的语言标注点云质量评估数据集，通过引入多级失真严重度标签、离散质量类别和结构化自然语言描述来增强可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PCQA方法仅预测标量平均意见分数(MOS)，量化整体感知质量但无法揭示具体失真原因。人类观察者则能自然识别特定失真类型(如模糊、色彩偏移、点密度变化、缺失区域和几何变形)，因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 引入DAL-PCQA数据集，包含：1)多级失真严重度标签；2)离散质量类别；3)与人类感知对齐的结构化自然语言描述；4)定义覆盖光度和几何伪影的点云特定失真分类法；5)统计分析揭示跨失真类型和质量级别的特征退化模式；6)比较零样本和微调多模态模型用于生成感知质量描述。

Result: 统计分析揭示了不同失真类型和质量级别的特征退化模式。实验表明，失真感知监督显著改善了生成描述与真实描述之间的词汇和语义对齐。数据集公开可用。

Conclusion: DAL-PCQA通过支持可解释的失真级别推理，促进了语言驱动、可解释的点云质量评估，弥合了传统标量评分方法与人类自然推理方式之间的差距。

Abstract: Point Cloud Quality Assessment (PCQA) methods typically predict scalar Mean Opinion Scores (MOS), which quantify overall perceptual degradation but do not reveal its causes. In contrast, human observers naturally reason in terms of specific distortions such as blur, color shifts, point density changes, missing regions, and geometric deformations. To close this gap, we introduce DAL-PCQA, a distortion-aware, language-annotated dataset for PCQA. DAL-PCQA augments benchmark point clouds with multi-level distortion severity labels, discrete quality categories, and structured natural language descriptions aligned with human perception. We define a point-cloud-specific distortion taxonomy that covers both photometric and geometric artifacts. Statistical analysis reveals characteristic degradation patterns across distortion types and quality levels. To assess the utility of these annotations, we compare zero-shot and fine-tuned multimodal models for generating perceptual quality descriptions. Experiments show that distortion-aware supervision substantially improves lexical and semantic alignment with ground-truth descriptions. By enabling interpretable, distortion-level reasoning, DAL-PCQA facilitates language-driven, explainable point cloud quality assessment. The dataset is publicly available at https://github.com/swarna96/DAL-PCQA.

</details>


### [48] [ChronoPhyBench: Do MLLMs Truly Understand the World or Merely Exploit Language Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07962)
*Bin Zhu,Yanhao Jia,Kexin Zhao,Jie Wang,Munan Ning,Hao Li,Yuwei Niu,Tanqing Sun,Huangchong Yan,Mingjun Pan,Xinyi Wu,Qishen Yin,Yunyang Ge,Shuai Zhao,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个名为ChronoPhyBench的新型多模态物理动态推理基准，旨在解决现有多模态大语言模型在跨模态推理中的歧义性问题，通过结合历史视频上下文和文本描述，要求模型预测后续物理状态，从而评估模型是否真正进行物理基础的多模态推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在开放世界推理和理解方面表现出色，但存在一个关键歧义：无法确定这些模型是真正合成了跨模态信息来构建物理基础的推理链，还是仅仅利用强大的语言先验来掩盖对单一模态的依赖，从而"幻觉"出高级的多模态能力。为了严格缓解语言模态偏差和捷径，需要开发新的评估框架。

Method: 提出了ChronoPhyBench基准，统一了下一状态预测与视觉问答范式，通过基于历史视频上下文和文本描述的条件化，强制模型通过单图像选择和多帧时间顺序排序这两种任务来推断后续物理状态。同时构建了一个大规模多模态推理数据集，包含超过10,000个长视频和精心标注的描述，总计500万tokens。

Result: 实验评估揭示了与先前基准结论的鲜明对比：当前开源模型执行物理基础多模态推理的能力仍处于起步阶段。该工作系统地压力测试了多模态模型的推理能力，量化了幻觉率，并推进了物理AI的发展。

Conclusion: 该研究为社区提供了一个稳健且透明的评估框架，旨在推动人工通用智能的发展，通过ChronoPhyBench基准系统性地评估多模态模型的物理推理能力，揭示当前模型的局限性，并为未来模型开发提供指导。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable proficiency in open-world reasoning and understanding. However, a critical ambiguity persists: it remains unclear whether these models genuinely synthesize cross-modal information to construct physically grounded reasoning chains, or if they merely exploit strong language priors to mask single-modality reliance, thereby hallucinating advanced multimodal capabilities. Motivated by this, and to rigorously mitigate language modality bias and shortcuts, we propose a novel multimodal Chrono}logical Physical Dynamics Reasoning Benchmark ChronoPhyBench, which unifies next state prediction with Visual Question Answering (VQA) paradigms by conditioning on historical video context and textual captions to enforce models to deduce subsequent physical states through both single image selection and the inherently more complex task of multiple frame chronological sorting. Concurrently, we construct a large-scale multimodal reasoning dataset curated using the ChronoPhyBench criteria, comprising over 10,000 long-form videos paired with meticulously annotated captions, totaling 5M tokens. Our experimental evaluations reveal a stark contrast to conclusions drawn by previous benchmarks. The capacity of current open-source models to perform physically grounded multimodal reasoning remains in its infancy. Ultimately, this work seeks to systematically stress-test the reasoning capabilities of multimodal models, quantify hallucination rates, and advance the development of Physical AI, thereby providing the community with a robust and transparent evaluation framework toward Artificial General Intelligence (AGI).

</details>


### [49] [DisCo: World Models with Discrete Camera Motion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07967)
*Hongrui Huang,Junke Wang,Quanhao Li,Yu-Gang Jiang,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisCo是一个可控视频世界模型，通过使用离散动作基元作为生成条件，解决了连续相机轨迹表示中的动作纠缠问题，显著提升了动作跟随的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控视频世界模型大多依赖连续相机轨迹作为动作条件，这在复杂运动序列下经常导致动作跟随不可靠。作者发现动作表示纠缠是关键瓶颈，连续相机表示导致不同运动模式之间特征相似度高，从而降低了动作可控性。

Method: 提出DisCo模型，将生成条件基于一组紧凑的离散动作基元，以改善动作可分离性。同时引入DisCoBench基准测试，用于评估模型在短期、长期和高度动态探索场景中的能力。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，DisCo在保持视觉质量的同时，实现了显著更可靠的动作跟随。

Conclusion: 使用离散动作基元作为生成条件可以有效解决动作表示纠缠问题，提升可控视频世界模型的交互探索能力。DisCoBench为评估这类模型提供了全面的基准框架。

Abstract: Controllable video world models target interactive world exploration, where models must faithfully execute explicit action commands while preserving visual quality and temporal coherence. However, most existing approaches rely on continuous camera trajectories as action conditions, which often lead to unreliable action following, especially under complex motion sequences. In this work, we identify action representation entanglement as a key bottleneck in controllable video generation, and show that continuous camera representations lead to high feature similarity across distinct motion patterns, degrading action controllability. Based on this insight, we propose DisCo, a controllable video world model that conditions generation on a compact set of discrete action primitives to improve action separability. We further introduce DisCoBench, a comprehensive benchmark for evaluating the ability of models in short-term, long-horizon, and highly dynamic exploration scenarios. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DisCo achieves significantly more reliable action following while preserving visual quality.

</details>


### [50] [FMRFusion: Frequency-Aware Multi-View Representation Learning for Heterogeneous Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07985)
*Tao Zhoua,Yunlong Liu,Qinghui Chen,Zekai Zhang,Minlong Sun,Changlin Biana,Dagang Li,Wenmin Wang,Jinglin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FMRFusion是一种频率感知的多视图表示学习网络，用于红外与可见光图像融合，通过多尺度结构感知、双线性频率分解和跨视图互补交互等技术，显著提升了融合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外与可见光图像融合旨在生成保留重要目标信息和纹理细节的复合图像。传统单模块堆叠方法对两种模态不同特性的学习不完整，限制了融合效果和在真实异构数据场景中的鲁棒性。

Method: 1. 引入多尺度结构感知模块捕获判别性结构；2. 采用双线性频率分解机制将特征分离为高频和低频分量；3. 融入跨视图互补交互显式建模反射光信息与辐射强度响应之间的互补特性；4. 使用流匹配技术逐步优化融合特征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，FMRFusion在多种融合任务中取得了优越且一致的性能，特别是在夜间场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: FMRFusion通过频率感知的多视图表示学习方法有效解决了红外与可见光图像融合中的挑战，显著提升了融合质量和鲁棒性，为异构图像融合提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Infrared and visible image fusion aims to generate a composite image that retains significant target information and preserves detailed textures, integrating two heterogeneous modalities. Previous image fusion methods typically adopt a single-module stacking approach to extract features from the two modalities. However, these approaches may result in incomplete learning of their distinct characteristics, thereby limiting the fusion effectiveness and constrain ing robustness in real-world heterogeneous data scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose FMRFusion, a frequency-aware multi-view representation learning network for Heterogeneous Image Fusion. A Multi-Scale Struc tural Perception Module is introduced to effectively capture discriminative structures, extracting fine-grained local structures and essential contextual information. A bilinear frequency decomposition mechanism is employed to sepa rate features into high-frequency and low-frequency components, enabling joint modeling of local details and global representations across different frequency domains. Moreover, a Cross-View Complementary Interaction is incorpo rated to explicitly model and fuse the complementary characteristics between reflected light information and radiative intensity responses, facilitating effective cross-view interaction. We further improve the Performance of the fused results by flow matching, which progressively refines the fused features by learning the transformation from coarse data to high-quality representations. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that FMRFusion achieves superior and consistent performance across a range of fusion tasks, especially in nighttime scenarios

</details>


### [51] [Learning a Semantic Calibration Network for Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08001)
*Yang Sun,Tao Wang,Anastasia Ioannou,Ge Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的语义校准网络（SCN），通过明确建模类别间的语义相关性来改进掩码分类过程，增强判别能力的同时有效保留预训练CLIP模型的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放词汇分割（OVS）需要从固定类别集扩展到开放集，基于任意文本输入识别和分割新概念。现有方法主要关注特征聚合或简单微调预训练模型，在保持CLIP模型泛化能力的同时提升判别能力方面存在不足

Method: SCN包含两个核心组件：类别消歧（CD）和逻辑融合（LF）。1）使用交叉注意力机制将文本嵌入转换为视觉感知的伪文本嵌入，获得增强的相似度分数；2）CD模块通过残差架构捕获隐式的类别间依赖关系以解决语义歧义；3）LF模块动态整合多方面的语义证据，确保模型实现鲁棒的语义共识

Result: 在主流基准测试上的综合实验结果表明，该方法相比最先进的算法取得了显著的性能提升

Conclusion: SCN通过明确建模语义相关性和动态融合多源语义证据，在增强模型判别能力的同时有效保留了预训练CLIP模型的泛化能力，为开放词汇语义分割提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Semantic image segmentation assigns a predefined category label to each pixel, has achieved significant progress lately. Open-Vocabulary Segmentation (OVS) extends the segmentation task from a fixed set to an open set, enabling the identification and segmentation of novel concepts based on arbitrary text inputs, such as category names or descriptions. In this paper, we propose a novel Semantic Calibration Network (SCN) for open-vocabulary semantic segmentation. Different from prior approaches that focus on feature aggregation or simple fine-tuning of pre-trained models, SCN refines the mask classification process by explicitly modeling the semantic correlations between classes, aiming to enhance the model's discriminative power while effectively preserving the generalization abilities of the pre-trained CLIP model. Specifically, SCN comprises two core components: Class Disambiguation (CD) and Logits Fusion (LF). First, a cross-attention mechanism is utilized to transform the text embeddings into visually aware pseudo-text embeddings, in order to derive an enhanced similarity score that complements the original mask-text similarity score. Subsequently, the Class Disambiguation module captures implicit inter-class dependencies through a residual architecture to effectively resolve semantic ambiguities. Finally, the Logits Fusion module dynamically integrates multifaceted semantic evidence to ensure that the model achieves a robust semantic consensus while maintaining CLIP's inherent generalization capability. Comprehensive experimental results on mainstream benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method achieves significant performance improvements compared to state-of-the-art algorithms.

</details>


### [52] [Aqua Boundary-Saliency Attention Module for Lightweight Underwater Salient Instance Segmentation Detection Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08002)
*M. Fazri Nizar,Julian Supardi,Muhammad Naufal Rachmatullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了LUSIS-DETR，一个轻量化的水下显著实例分割检测变换器框架，通过Aqua边界-显著性注意模块整合水下视觉线索，实现高效实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于提示和大规模基础模型的水下实例分割方法虽然提升了掩码质量，但依赖大型模型、提示生成或额外模态估计，导致部署效率低下，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 设计了轻量级检测变换器框架LUSIS-DETR，核心是Aqua边界-显著性注意模块（AquaBSAM），该模块将水下边界、对比度、衰减、色度、暗通道和中心先验线索通过边界残差调制嵌入DINOv2初始化的多尺度特征中。训练时使用辅助掩码监督和小目标复制粘贴技术。

Result: 在四个近期水下实例分割数据集（UIIS、UIIS10K、USIS10K、USIS16K）上，在类别感知和显著实例协议下均表现出竞争领先性能。在NVIDIA T4 GPU上使用TensorRT半精度（FP16）推理延迟为4.31-6.34毫秒，支持实时推理。

Conclusion: LUSIS-DETR是一个高效轻量化的水下实例分割框架，通过AquaBSAM整合水下特异性视觉线索，在保持高性能的同时实现实时部署，解决了现有方法部署复杂的问题。

Abstract: Underwater instance segmentation integrates pixel-level mask prediction and instance-level discrimination for marine resource exploration, ecological monitoring, and underwater robotic perception. Recent prompt-based and auxiliary-modality methods improve mask quality, but their reliance on large foundation models, prompt generation, or extra modality estimation complicates efficient deployment. This work introduces Lightweight Underwater Salient Instance Segmentation Detection Transformer (LUSIS-DETR), a compact detection-transformer framework built around the Aqua Boundary-Saliency Attention Module (AquaBSAM). AquaBSAM embeds underwater boundary, contrast, attenuation, chroma, dark-channel, and center-prior cues into DINOv2-initialized multi-scale features through bounded residual modulation, while auxiliary mask supervision and small-object copy-paste are training-only. Extensive evaluation on four recent underwater instance segmentation datasets, UIIS, UIIS10K, USIS10K, and USIS16K, shows competitively leading performance against previous state-of-the-art works across category-aware and salient-instance protocols. TensorRT half-precision (FP16) benchmarking on an NVIDIA T4 graphics processing unit (GPU) achieves 4.31-6.34 milliseconds (ms) latency, supporting real-time inference under an accessible reproduction setting.

</details>

### [53] [Bidirectional Small-Granularity Search between Code and Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07519)
*Marco A. Valenzuela-Escárcega,Enrique Noriega-Atala,Gus Hahn-Powell,Clayton T. Morrison,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了代码与文本间双向细粒度搜索的新任务，创建了包含GPT-4生成训练数据和手动标注测试数据的大型数据集，并提出共享编码器的模块化方法，在域内表现良好，域外结果令人鼓舞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏在科学出版物中建立文本描述与对应代码片段之间直接关联的细粒度搜索能力，这阻碍了对科学方法的更好、更快理解。需要一种能够在小片段代码和文本之间进行双向搜索的解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出了双向细粒度搜索任务：查询是小片段文本或代码，结果是对立模态的小片段
2. 构建大型数据集：训练分区使用GPT-4自动生成代码的文本描述；测试分区包含一个域内和两个域外（OOD）数据集，含手动标注和其他领域材料
3. 提出模块化方法：共享编码器处理四个子任务，学习双向答案跨度的开始/结束位置

Result: 1. 方法在域内数据集上取得了良好的结果
2. 在域外（OOD）测试集上获得了令人鼓舞的结果
3. 表明使用自动生成数据解决此任务是可行的

Conclusion: 通过自动生成数据解决代码与文本间双向细粒度搜索任务是可行的，但仍有许多激动人心的未来工作需要完成。该方法为科学出版物中文本描述与代码片段建立直接联系提供了有效途径。

Abstract: We introduce the novel task of bidirectional small-granularity search between code and text, where the queries are small snippets of text or code and the results are also small fragments of the opposite modality, i.e., code or text. This task establishes direct links between text in scientific publications and corresponding code segments, in support of better and faster understanding of scientific methods. We introduce a large dataset for the proposed task that includes a training partition with textual descriptions of code generated automatically using GPT-4, and three testing partitions, one in-domain and two out-of-domain (OOD) that contain manually-annotated data as well as material from other domains. We also propose a modular approach to address this task. Our approach shares an encoder across four different subtasks that learn start/end of answer spans in both directions. We show that our method achieves good results in-domain, and encouraging results OOD. This suggests that addressing this task with automatically-generated data is possible, but there is exciting future work to be done.

</details>


### [54] [TinyJudge: Unverifiable Constraint Alignment via Lightweight Specialist Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07520)
*Yirong Zeng,Yufei Liu,Xiao Ding,Yutai Hou,Yuxian Wang,Wu Ning,Haonan Song,Dandan Tu,Qixun Zhang,Yuxiang He,Bibo Cai,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TinyJudge框架使用小型语言模型(约0.6B)集合来评估不可验证约束，为强化学习提供奖励，在性能、奖励精度和训练速度上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前使用LLM-as-a-judge评估不可验证约束的方法存在严重奖励欺骗和计算开销大的问题，成为指令跟随任务的重要瓶颈。

Method: 首先分析不可验证约束的泛化能力，发现特定约束具有高泛化模式；然后提出TinyJudge框架，通过从前沿模型蒸馏专业知识到专门的小型语言模型(约0.6B)集合，为软约束提供奖励评估。

Result: 在五个基准测试中，TinyJudge比基线平均性能提高约10%，奖励精度提高12%，总训练时间加速3倍。

Conclusion: TinyJudge为对齐LLM与不可验证的人类指令提供了一条可扩展且稳健的路径。

Abstract: Instruction Following (IF) is a core capability of LLMs, requiring strict adherence to diverse constraints, ranging from verifiable ones (e.g., output length) to unverifiable ones (e.g., tone). Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has emerged as a paradigm for IF tasks, leveraging LLM-as-a-judge to assess unverifiable constraints. However, we empirically find that this approach remains a significant bottleneck, suffering from severe reward hacking and higher computational overhead. In this work, we first analyze the generalization capabilities of unverifiable constraints and discover that specific constraints exhibit distinct, high-generalization patterns. Motivated by this, we propose TinyJudge, a framework that employs an ensemble of specialized tiny language models ($\sim0.6B$) to provide rewards for soft constraints. By distilling expertise from frontier models into these tiny models, it achieves high-precision, lightweight evaluation. Extensive evaluations across five benchmarks demonstrate that TinyJudge outperforms the baselines by $\sim10\%$ in average performance and $12\%$ in reward precision. Crucially, it also achieves a $3\times$ speedup in total training time. Our work provides a scalable and robust path for aligning LLMs with unverifiable human instructions.

</details>


### [55] [Evaluating Hallucinations in Domain-Adapted Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07521)
*Sanchita Porwal,Sai Prasath S,Xingjian Bi,Madelyn Scandlen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究调查了领域适应大语言模型（LLM）中的幻觉现象，发现仅通过微调Llama-2模型在Lamini数据集上无法有效缓解幻觉问题，特别是在处理新领域特定信息时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索领域适应大语言模型中出现的幻觉问题。当LLM使用领域特定数据进行微调时，容易产生无意义或不忠实的内容，这构成了一个重要挑战。

Method: 研究方法包括一系列实验，测试微调后LLM的记忆、回忆和推理能力。通过对比模型在新问答对和领域特定信息上的表现，评估其幻觉倾向。

Result: 研究发现模型在类似训练数据的任务上表现良好，但在准确推理和回忆新领域特定信息方面能力有限，导致幻觉产生。模型倾向于提供正确回答但附带额外信息，显示出过度生成的倾向。

Conclusion: 结论表明仅靠微调方法在缓解LLM领域适应中的幻觉问题存在重要局限性，需要更稳健的领域适应方法。研究还揭示了LLM在不同类型信息上表现的差异，特别是在处理领域特定查询方面的相对弱点。

Abstract: This study investigates the phenomenon of hallucinations in domain-adapted Large Language Models (LLMs), focusing on the fine-tuning of the Llama-2 model with the Lamini dataset. Hallucinations, or the generation of nonsensical or unfaithful content by LLMs, pose a significant challenge, especially when these models are fine-tuned with domain-specific data. Our methodology involves a series of experiments testing memorization, recall, and reasoning capabilities of the fine-tuned LLM, comparing its performance on novel question-answer pairs and domain-specific information. We found that while the model shows proficiency in tasks similar to its training data, its capability to accurately reason about and recall new domain-specific information remains limited, leading to instances of hallucination. The model demonstrates a tendency to provide correct answers with extra information, suggesting an inclination toward over-generation. These results suggest important limitations of fine-tuning-only approaches for mitigating hallucinations when adapting LLMs to specialized domains and underscore the need for more robust methods in adapting LLMs to specialized domains. The study also provides insights into the varying performance of LLMs on different types of information, revealing a comparative weakness in handling domain-specific queries.

</details>


### [56] [Community-Specific Slang and Entity Detection via Semantic Shift in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07522)
*Julia Kruk,Sanchita Porwal,Amitrajit Bhattacharjee,Mansi Phute*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种无监督方法，通过测量语义偏移量来识别在线社区中的俚语、独特实体和民俗文化术语，该方法基于预训练LLM在社区特定语料库微调前后的词向量余弦相似度差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线社区常使用独特的俚语、实体和民俗术语，这些术语对社区外人士难以理解。需要自动化方法来识别这些社区特有词汇，以更好地理解社区文化和语言演变。

Method: 定义语义偏移为预训练LLM在社区特定语料库微调前后词向量表示的变化程度，通过余弦相似度量化。使用DistilRoBERTa模型，在三个Reddit子社区（r/Technology、r/Gaming、r/WorldofWarcraft）语料库上微调，建立词汇表余弦相似度分布，提取底部10%相似度的词汇作为社区特有术语。

Result: 通过分布分析，底部10%百分位的词汇能够成功识别出对社区具有独特意义的术语，而顶部10%百分位的词汇则包含相对通用的语义内容。验证了基于语义偏移的方法可以有效分离社区特有词汇。

Conclusion: 提出的无监督方法通过量化语义偏移能有效识别在线社区特有词汇，为社区语言分析提供了自动化工具，有助于理解社区文化和语言演变模式。

Abstract: We propose an unsupervised method of resolving slang, unique entities, and folklore from online communities by isolating words in the lexicon that have the highest magnitude of semantic shift. Semantic shift is defined as the evolution of a word's encoded representation as a result of fine-tuning a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM) on a community-specific text corpus. This value is inversely proportional to the cosine similarity between the base model's encoded representation of a word, and a fine-tuned model's encoded representation. We fine-tune the DistilRoBERTa model on text corpora collected from 3 Reddit subreddits (r/Technology, r/Gaming, r/WorldofWarcraft), model a distribution of cosine similarity over the lexicon, and show that one can successfully resolve words that have unique significance to the community by pulling data in the bottom 10-percentile. In contrast, we show that data in the top 10-percentile consist of words that carry relatively universal semantics.

</details>


### [57] [Retrieval Augmented Generation Framework for the Nepali Legal Domain Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07523)
*Samir Wagle,Abiral Adhikari,Reewaj Khanal,Batsal Bhandari,Prashant Manandhar,Praveen Acharya,Bal Krishna Bal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了首个基于检索增强生成(RAG)的尼泊尔语法律问答系统，利用从尼泊尔法律公报数字档案中提取的案例法，有效解决了尼泊尔语作为低资源语言在法律人工智能应用中的数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高资源语言（如英语）的法律领域已广泛采用人工智能进行法律问答，但低资源语言如尼泊尔语因数据稀缺限制了在尼泊尔法律文本上训练大型语言模型，阻碍了尼泊尔语法律AI系统的发展。

Method: 采用检索增强生成(RAG)框架，使用BM25算法对分块文档进行检索，并结合多语言E5大型模型。从尼泊尔法律公报数字档案中提取案例法作为知识源，构建尼泊尔语法律问答系统。

Result: BM25检索在Top-1准确率达到91%，多语言E5模型达到75%。生成答案的评估显示：基于BM25文档检索时，回答的接地性为74%，自动评估模型判断的真实性为85%，人工评估的真实性为84%，成功生成回答率为92%。

Conclusion: RAG管道能够有效解决低资源语言法律问答的空白，为尼泊尔语法律领域可靠的人工智能系统提供了基础，证明了在数据稀缺情况下通过检索增强方法构建有效法律问答系统的可行性。

Abstract: Legal domains in high-resource languages like English have widely adopted artificial intelligence for legal question answering. However, data scarcity in low resource languages such as Nepali has limited the training of large language models on Nepali legal texts. This study presents the first application of a Retrieval Augmented Generation based model for Nepali legal question answering using case laws extracted from the Nepal Kanun Patrika digital archive. Using BM25 on chunked documents, the approach achieved a top precision at one of 91 percent, and up to 75 percent with the multilingual E5 large model. Evaluation of generated answers showed 74 percent groundedness, 85 percent truthfulness according to an automated judge model, and 84 percent human evaluated truthfulness when using BM25 document retrieval, with a 92 percent successful answer generation rate. These results demonstrate that the RAG pipeline can effectively address the gap in legal question answering for low resource languages and provide a foundation for reliable AI systems in the Nepali legal domain.

</details>


### [58] [ABLE: Representing and Mapping LLMs via Attribution-Based Large-model Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07524)
*Zirui Wang,Yusen Hou,Shaofeng Liang,Bowen Tian,Yanlin Zhang,Wenshuo Chen,Yutao Yue*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ABLE是一个利用可解释性空间构建大语言模型表示的框架，通过基于梯度的特征归因和分词器无关的词级对齐来捕捉模型特定的输入敏感模式，无需训练即可实现有效的模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生态系统的异构性和缺乏文档使得系统性的模型比较变得重要，但现有表示方法存在不足：基于内部参数的方法在架构兼容性下有效但面临可扩展性障碍；基于外部输出的方法可能混淆行为相似的模型且难以在不同分词器间对齐。

Method: 提出ABLE框架，利用可解释性空间构建模型表示：通过聚合基于梯度的特征归因，使用分词器无关的词级对齐方法，捕捉模型特定的输入敏感模式而非仅表面输出。

Result: 在239个开源LLM上进行广泛实验，显示这种无需训练的方法在关系预测、模型路由和基准分数预测任务上达到竞争性或优于现有方法的性能。

Conclusion: ABLE通过可解释性空间有效解决了异构LLM生态系统中的模型比较问题，具有理论稳定性保证和实际应用价值。

Abstract: The explosive growth of large language models (LLMs) has created a heterogeneous and poorly documented ecosystem, making systematic model comparison increasingly important for provenance auditing, security analysis, and model selection. Existing representation methods struggle to address this setting efficiently. Approaches analyzing internal parameters are powerful when architectures are compatible, but face scalability barriers under structural heterogeneity, while methods relying on external outputs may conflate models with similar behaviors and are difficult to align in richer output spaces across different tokenizers. To bridge this gap, we propose ABLE (Attribution-Based Large-model Embedding), a framework that leverages the interpretability space to construct model representations. By aggregating gradient-based feature attributions via a tokenizer-agnostic word-level alignment, ABLE captures model-specific input-sensitivity patterns rather than only surface-level outputs. Beyond empirical utility, we provide a stability analysis showing that, under standard regularity assumptions for differentiable Transformer-style models, ABLE induces a Lipschitz-continuous parameter-to-embedding map with finite-sample convergence guarantees. Extensive experiments on 239 open-source LLMs demonstrate that our training-free approach achieves competitive or superior performance in relation prediction, model routing, and benchmark score prediction.

</details>


### [59] [Implicit Causal Graph Construction in Text via Chain Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07525)
*Liesbeth Allein,Marie-Francine Moens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大语言模型从文本中推断隐含的因果图，通过将描述的因果对视为潜在因果图的起点和终点，并推断中间因果事件。比较端到端图构建与因果链发现方法，探索群体智慧的扩展，并使用包含1,560个科学验证因果对的数据库进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本中的因果图通常只包含可观测的、预定义的事件，而本文旨在研究从文本中构建隐含的因果图。传统方法只能处理显式的因果关系，无法揭示潜在的、未明确表述的中间因果机制。这限制了深度理解文本中复杂因果关系的可能性。

Method: 1. 将每个描述的因果对视为潜在因果图的起点和终点
2. 使用大语言模型推断中间因果事件
3. 比较两种主要方法：端到端图构建和因果链发现
4. 因果链发现包括：聚合推断的链，以及通过迭代搜索过程逐步扩展部分链
5. 探索群体智慧扩展：事后聚合设置和协作推断设置，访问多个LLMs的因果知识

Result: 提出了一种基于数据库的评估方法，使用包含1,560个科学验证因果对的数据库来评估推断因果关系的有效性。这种评估被证明是可靠的、资源高效的，并且可转移到没有地面真实图的设置中。分析了不同方法之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种从文本中构建隐含因果图的新范式，通过LLMs推断中间事件来丰富因果表示。提出的数据库评估方法为缺乏真实图的情况提供了可行的评估方案，为因果推理研究提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Causal graphs in text are typically populated by observable, predefined events. In contrast, we study implicit causal graph construction from text by treating each described cause-effect pair as the begin- and endpoint of an underlying latent causal graph and using large language models (LLMs) to infer intermediate causal events. We compare end-to-end graph construction with methods that frame the task as causal chain discovery. In the latter, graphs are built either by aggregating inferred chains or by progressively expanding partial chains through an iterative search process. We further explore Wisdom of the Crowd extensions that access causal knowledge from multiple LLMs in post-hoc aggregation and collaborative inference settings. We analyze trade-offs among these approaches and evaluate the validity of inferred causal relations using a manually curated database of 1,560 scientifically validated causal pairs. This database-based evaluation is proposed as reliable, resource-efficient, and transferable to settings where ground-truth graphs are unavailable.

</details>


### [60] [GraphLoRA: Structure-Aware Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Model Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07526)
*Lin Mu,Guoji Wang,Li Ni,Lei Sang,Zhize Wu,Peiquan Jin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GraphLoRA是一个将低秩适配从独立扩展到结构感知传播的新框架，通过在LoRA路径中嵌入可训练图消息传递网络，使结构信号通过参数空间传播，实现图结构与文本语义的深度融合，显著提升LLM-based推荐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based推荐方法虽然展示了强大潜力，但难以有效对齐LLM建模的文本语义与协同信号。现有方法要么将协同信息转换为文本提示，要么将预训练嵌入注入LLM，都只能将结构信息作为静态输入处理，无法捕捉高阶关系依赖。

Method: 提出GraphLoRA框架，将低秩适配从独立推广到结构感知传播。在低秩适配路径中嵌入可训练的图消息传递网络，使结构信号通过参数空间传播。这种设计让协同拓扑显式指导参数更新，促进图结构和文本语义信息的深度整合。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，GraphLoRA不仅优于最先进的基于LLM的推荐方法，而且实现了卓越的泛化能力，有效平衡了结构推理能力与计算效率。代码已在GitHub上开源。

Conclusion: GraphLoRA通过将图消息传递整合到低秩适配路径中，成功解决了LLM-based推荐中文本语义与协同信号对齐的挑战，实现了结构信息与文本语义的深度集成，为LLMRec领域提供了一种高效且强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential for recommendation (LLMRec) due to their powerful reasoning and generalization abilities. However, effectively aligning the textual semantics modeled by LLMs with the collaborative signals remains a key challenge. Existing methods either translate collaborative information into textual prompts or inject pre-trained embeddings into the LLM, both of which treat structural information as static input and fail to capture high-order relational dependencies. To bridge this gap, we propose GraphLoRA, a novel framework that generalizes low-rank adaptation from independent to structure-aware propagation. GraphLoRA embeds a trainable graph message-passing network within the low-rank adaptation pathway, enabling structural signals to propagate through the parameter space. This design allows collaborative topology to explicitly guide parameter updates, fostering deep integration between graph-structured and textual semantic information. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that GraphLoRA not only outperforms state-of-the-art LLM-based recommendation methods but also achieves superior generalization, effectively balancing structural reasoning capability with computational efficiency. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/wgj15965/GraphLoRA}{https://github.com/wgj15965/GraphLoRA}.

</details>


### [61] [Post-training is (Massive) Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07527)
*Michael Hassid,Yossi Adi,Roy Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文认为当前LLM训练范式（大规模SFT和RL后训练）实际上回归到了BERT时代的"预训练-微调"模式，只是将模型适配到特定基准测试，而非真正的通用能力发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM训练范式过度依赖大规模后训练（SFT和RL），这实际上是一种向BERT时代"预训练-微调"模式的倒退，只是将模型专门适配到特定基准测试，而非发展真正的通用能力。

Method: 1. 提供LLM发展的历史概述；2. 通过实验比较预训练模型和随机初始化模型，在现代化推理数据集上进行微调；3. 在竞争性数学和代码基准上评估两种变体；4. 分析后训练方法作为分布拟合机制的作用。

Result: 从零开始后训练的模型表现出高度非平凡的性能，表明当前的后训练方法主要作为分布拟合机制运作，而非发展基础能力。

Conclusion: 开发真正通用的模型和系统需要超越为预定义行为进行的大规模后训练，转向让模型"学习如何学习"的训练过程，这代表了更根本的范式转变。

Abstract: The prevailing paradigm for training LLMs has evolved to rely on a massive post-training phase consisting of SFT and RL. In this position paper, we argue that this methodology effectively marks a reversion to the ``pre-train then fine-tune'' approach of the BERT era, explicitly tailoring models to the desired behaviors and specific benchmarks on which they are evaluated. We begin with a historical overview of LLMs, describing the different phases of the LLM evolution. We argue that the current landscape is remarkably similar to the early days of LLMs, where task performance heavily relied on fitting the models to in-distribution datasets. To empirically demonstrate this, we compare pre-trained models to randomly initialized ones, by fine-tuning both variants on modern reasoning datasets and evaluating them on competitive math and code benchmarks. We show that models post-trained from scratch yield highly non-trivial performance. Our findings suggest that current post-training methodologies function primarily as a distribution-fitting mechanism. We finish by positing that developing generally capable models and systems requires moving beyond extensive post-training for predefined behaviors, shifting instead toward training procedures where models ``learn how to learn''.

</details>


### [62] [BEACON: Behavioral Entropy Aggregation for Cross-Model Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07528)
*Naveen Bera,Pulijala Sai Nikhila,Kondaguduru Abhiram,Shaik Gayaz Ali,Shoaib Sadiq Salehmohamed,Shaik Mohammed Omar,Jinal Prashant Thakkar,Hansika Aredla,Shalmali Ayachit*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BEACON是一个纯黑盒的LLM幻觉检测框架，通过提取31维特征向量（包括语义熵、嵌入几何、思维链一致性和释义稳定性信号），使用梯度提升分类器在7个基准的7,617个标记样本上达到0.8123 AUROC，优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的幻觉（生成事实上不正确或未经支持的内容）仍然是可靠部署的关键障碍。现有方法通常需要访问内部表示或外部知识库，而BEACON旨在开发一种纯黑盒的幻觉检测框架，仅基于模型输出，无需内部访问。

Method: BEACON是一个黑盒幻觉检测框架，通过结构化多遍生成提取31维特征向量。这些特征包括：NLI基础的语义熵、嵌入几何、思维链一致性和释义稳定性信号。然后使用梯度提升分类器进行检测，并在7个基准的7,617个标记样本上进行训练。

Result: BEACON在7个基准测试中实现了0.8123 ± 0.0102 AUROC（95%置信区间：0.7632-0.8251），显著优于独立语义熵基线（+0.2298）和SelfCheckGPT风格的一致性基线（+0.2457）。一个高效的5次调用变体达到0.7795 AUROC，可实现实际部署。

Conclusion: BEACON展示了在黑盒设置下有效检测LLM幻觉的能力，特征重要性分析表明幻觉是多维的，需要结合多种不确定性信号。该框架具有实际部署价值，无需访问模型内部或外部知识库。

Abstract: Hallucination in large language models (LLMs), defined as the generation of factually incorrect or unsupported content, remains a critical barrier to reliable deployment. We present BEACON (Behavioral Entropy Aggregation for Cross-model hallucination detectiON), a black-box hallucination detection framework that operates purely on model outputs without requiring access to internal representations or external knowledge bases. BEACON extracts a 31-dimensional feature vector from structured multi-pass generation, integrating NLI-based semantic entropy, embedding geometry, chain-of-thought consistency, and paraphrase stability signals. A gradient-boosted classifier trained on 7,617 labeled examples across seven benchmarks achieves 0.8123 +/- 0.0102 AUROC (95% CI: 0.7632-0.8251), outperforming standalone semantic entropy (+0.2298) and SelfCheckGPT-style consistency baselines (+0.2457). Feature importance analysis shows that hallucination is inherently multi-dimensional, requiring combined uncertainty signals. An efficient 5-call variant achieves 0.7795 AUROC, enabling practical deployment across black-box LLM APIs.

</details>


### [63] [CAPruner: Conceptual-Adjacent Scene Graph Pruner for Enhancing 3D Spatial Reasoning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07529)
*Shengli Zhou,Xiangchen Wang,Guanhua Chen,Feng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAPruner：一种基于概念-空间相邻性的场景图剪枝方法，通过融合语义相关性和空间邻近性来选择对3D视觉语言任务关键的关系，显著提升大语言模型的空间推理性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉语言任务中，使用完整场景图进行推理会导致高昂的token成本和计算效率低下，需要进行剪枝。但现有剪枝方法主要依赖空间邻近性，往往会剪掉任务相关的关系，从而损害可靠的空间推理能力。

Method: 提出概念-相邻场景图剪枝器(CAPruner)，通过整合模糊语义相关性和空间邻近性来估计关系的重要性，从而在任务特定上下文中选择关键关系。为了避免昂贵的关系级标注，通过监督每个节点入射边的聚合分数来训练CAPruner。

Result: 大量实验表明，CAPruner能有效保留对空间推理至关重要的关系，使大语言模型在3D-VL任务上获得显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 场景图剪枝的关键需求是保留与特定3D-VL任务最相关的空间关系。CAPruner通过结合语义和空间信息，实现了任务感知的剪枝，显著提高了3D视觉语言任务的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently been applied to 3D vision-language (3D-VL) tasks, which require spatial reasoning to identify target objects relative to anchors. Scene graphs are commonly employed to represent such relations, but reasoning over complete graphs incurs high token costs and computational inefficiencies, motivating the need for pruning. Existing pruning methods primarily rely on spatial proximity and often remove task-relevant relations, thereby undermining reliable spatial reasoning. To address these limitations, we derive a key requirement for scene graph pruning: preserving spatial relations that are most pertinent to the specific 3D-VL task. Guided by this insight, we propose the Conceptual-Adjacent Scene Graph Pruner (CAPruner). CAPruner integrates fuzzy semantic relevance with spatial proximity to estimate the importance of relations, enabling the selection of critical relations in a task-specific context. Moreover, to avoid costly relation-level annotations, CAPruner is trained by supervising the aggregated scores of each node's incident edges. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CAPruner effectively preserves relations essential for spatial reasoning, leading to substantial performance improvements of LLMs on 3D-VL tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/fz-zsl/CAPruner.

</details>


### [64] [Finding New Connections between Concepts from Medline Database Incorporating Domain Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07530)
*Yang Weikang,Chowdhury S. M. Mazharul Hoque,Jin Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究基于Swanson的ABC模型，开发了一个用于发现医学概念间隐藏连接的文献挖掘模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据之间存在隐藏的相互关联，即使表面上看似无关的主题，实际上可能通过中间主题相互连接。在医学领域，发现这些隐藏关联对于知识发现和创新至关重要。

Method: 采用并改进Swanson的ABC模型（文献发现模型），该模型通过中间概念B连接看似无关的概念A和C，构建自适应模型来发现医学概念间的隐藏关系。

Result: 模型能够识别医学概念间的隐藏连接，证明表面上无关的医学主题可以通过共同的相关主题相互关联，揭示了医学知识间的潜在联系网络。

Conclusion: 文献发现模型在识别医学概念隐藏关联方面具有重要价值，有助于推动医学知识发现和创新，特别是在发现跨领域医学联系方面。

Abstract: In this digital world, data is everything and significantly impacts our everyday lives. Interestingly, in this small world, everything is part of an ecosystem, where everything is connected, directly or indirectly. The same thing happens to data as well. In most cases, it may seem like a particular topic does not have any connection with another one, but in reality, they are connected through a mutually related topic. Therefore, in this research, we will discuss an adaptive model modified from the ABC model by Don R. Swanson, a Literature-Based Discovery (LBD) Model, to find the hidden connections between Concepts of Interest. The model demonstrates that two topics, A and C are different and have no relationship. But they have a common topic, B that can be used to connect topics A and C This famous model will be used in this discussion to connect Medical Concepts.

</details>


### [65] [mllm-shap: A Shapley Value Explainability Platform for Text-Audio Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07531)
*Jakub Muszyński,Paweł Pozorski,Maria Ganzha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: mllm-shap是一个开源Python框架，将Shapley值可解释性从纯文本LLM扩展到处理文本和音频输入的多模态LLM，解决了模态间交互、多轮对话跟踪和计算复杂度等关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Shapley值可解释性研究主要集中于文本模型，而多模态LLM（特别是文本-音频模型）面临独特的挑战：需要处理离散文本标记和密集音频编码帧的混合输入、维护多轮对话上下文，以及长音频带来的计算复杂度问题。

Method: 提出了三个关键技术：1) 模态感知联盟掩蔽管理文本和音频的交叉处理；2) 多轮对话跟踪使用每标记元数据维护角色和模态上下文；3) 基于语音对齐的标记分组技术，将联盟空间减少10-50倍。框架实现了五种Shapley值估计策略，包括具有Neyman最优分配的互补贡献估计器。

Result: mllm-shap作为pip可安装包提供，包含基于Web的交互式GUI用于细粒度归因可视化。语音对齐标记分组技术使长音频的Shapley值估计在计算上可行，CC估计器比标准蒙特卡洛基线表现出更优的收敛性。

Conclusion: 这是第一个为文本-音频多模态LLM提供完整、可复现的Shapley值可解释性管道的公开可用框架，解决了多模态可解释性的关键挑战，并为研究人员和从业者提供了实用的工具。

Abstract: We introduce mllm-shap, an open-source Python framework designed to extend Shapley Value (SV) explainability from text-only Large Language Models to Multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) processing joint text and audio inputs. While text-based attribution is well-studied, mllm-shap addresses three critical challenges unique to the multimodal regime:
  (1) Modality-aware coalition masking, which manages the interleaved processing of discrete text tokens and dense audio encoder frames.
  (2) Multi-turn conversation tracking, utilizing per-token metadata to maintain role and modality context.
  (3) Phonetic alignment-based token grouping, a novel technique that reduces the coalition space by 10x to 50x, rendering SV estimation computationally feasible for long-form audio.
  The platform implements five SV estimation strategies, including a Complementary Contributions (CC) estimator with Neyman-optimal allocation that demonstrates superior convergence over standard Monte Carlo baselines. mllm-shap is provided as a pip-installable package featuring an interactive web-based GUI for granular attribution visualization. To our knowledge, this is the first publicly available framework providing a complete, reproducible pipeline for SV-based explainability in text-audio MLLMs.

</details>


### [66] [Principled Agent Debate: Adversarial Arbitration for Sycophancy Reduction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07532)
*Sam Ryan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Principled Agent Debate (PAD)是一种多智能体架构，通过仲裁两个具有相反哲学倾向的模型来减少RLHF训练模型中的系统性"附和偏误"，在SycophancyEval基准测试中显著优于单模型基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF训练的模型存在系统性偏误，倾向于附和用户观点而非追求准确性。这种"附和偏误"是训练过程的结构性属性，需要通过创新的多智能体架构来缓解。

Method: 提出PAD架构：1) 两个模型分别调整至相反的哲学倾向（静态倾向调整）；2) 独立进行单轮论证；3) 身份剥离后进行合成；4) 实用主义合成器盲目仲裁。评估了五种具体实现（AnCifer、DeWin、FeynStein、BurGal、Trident）。

Result: 在200个分层问题(SycophancyEval)上的评估显示：所有PAD变体显著优于单模型基线(18.5%)和指令对立基线(29.0%)，其中DeWin达到48.5%准确率(z=6.36, p<0.001)。变体间在n=200时无显著差异。BurGal变体达到53.0%但作为架构有效性检查。

Conclusion: PAD架构有效缓解了身份框架下的附和偏误。预训练底限影响了约40%的问题，未来的研究重点是微调倾向模型。多智能体辩论方法为减少RLHF模型系统性偏误提供了有前景的方向。

Abstract: RLHF-trained models are systematically biased toward agreement over accuracy, a structural property of the training process. We present Principled Agent Debate (PAD), a multi-agent architecture that mitigates identity-framed sycophancy by arbitrating between two models tuned to opposing philosophical dispositions, with a pragmatist synthesizer evaluating both arguments blind to their origins. This paper evaluates a prompt-based instantiation of PAD. The key mechanisms are static dispositional tuning, identity stripping before synthesis, single-round independent argumentation, and blind arbitration. We evaluate five instantiations on 200 stratified questions from SycophancyEval. All PAD variants (AnCifer, DeWin, FeynStein, BurGal, Trident) significantly outperform the single-model baseline (18.5%) and instructed-opposition baseline (29.0%), with DeWin achieving 48.5% accuracy (z=6.36, p<0.001 versus both). The variants are not significantly different from each other at n=200. The BurGal variant achieves 53.0% but functions as an architectural validity check; its consensus/heterodox axis structurally favors the heterodox model on every benchmark question. A pre-training floor affects an estimated 40% of questions; fine-tuned disposition models are the identified next step.

</details>


### [67] [Bridging Traditional Explainability Methods and Multimodal Multilingual Models: An XAI-Based Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07533)
*Paweł Pozorski,Jakub Muszyński,Maria Ganzha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一个多模态Shapley值框架，用于解释多模态大语言模型在文本-音频对话中的决策机制，解决了跨模态依赖性和计算复杂性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然多模态大语言模型能有效整合文本和音频理解复杂对话，但其内部异构模态如何影响模型行为的机制仍不透明。现有基于Shapley值的文本解释方法难以扩展到多模态数据，面临跨通道依赖、复杂对话结构和密集音频表示的高计算复杂度等挑战。

Method: 1) 形式化多模态Shapley值框架，将离散文本token和对齐的音频段作为协作特征；2) 采用高效估计策略：低维输入的精确SV计算，以及基于抽样的近似方法（蒙特卡洛置换和分层抽样）；3) 提出频谱图引导的语音对齐方法，将高频音频流映射到可解释的单词对齐段；4) 开发开源Python包和GUI进行多模态归因计算和可视化。

Result: 使用VoiceBench和Infinity Instruct数据集的精选子集进行多语言场景评估，实验结果表明：输入模态是归因波动的主要驱动因素，标准的句法重要性代理在多模态跨语言环境中往往无法预测模型注意力。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个可扩展的多模态解释框架，揭示了多模态大语言模型中异构模态交互的复杂动态，为理解和诊断多模态模型的决策过程提供了重要工具和方法。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) effectively integrate text and audio to interpret context in complex interactive dialogues. However, the internal mechanisms by which heterogeneous modalities influence model behavior remain opaque. While Shapley Values (SV) provide a robust, model-agnostic framework for local explainability in text-based NLP, their extension to multimodal data is hindered by cross-channel dependencies, intricate dialogue structures, and the prohibitive computational complexity of dense audio representations.
  In this work, we formalize a multimodal extension of the Shapley Value framework, treating discrete text tokens and aligned audio segments as cooperative features. To ensure computational feasibility, we deploy a suite of efficient estimation strategies: exact SV computation for low-dimensional inputs and sampling-based approximations - including Monte Carlo permutations and stratified sampling with Neyman-optimal allocation - to minimize variance under constrained computational budgets. To resolve the granularity mismatch between modalities, we propose Spectrogram-Guided Phonetic Alignment (SGPA), a novel preprocessing method that maps high-frequency audio streams to interpretable, word-aligned segments.
  Our contribution is twofold: first, we provide an open-source, model-agnostic Python package and a companion GUI for the computation and interactive visualization of multimodal attributions. Second, we evaluate our framework using curated subsets of the VoiceBench and Infinity Instruct datasets across diverse multilingual scenarios. Our experimental results reveal that input modality is a primary driver of attribution volatility and demonstrate that standard syntactic importance proxies often fail to predict model attention in multimodal, cross-lingual contexts.

</details>


### [68] [Multilingual Refusal Alignment for Safer Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07535)
*Aleksandra Krasnodębska,Wojciech Kusa,Aldo Lipani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多语言大语言模型的安全对齐问题，揭示了仅英语对齐不足以确保跨语言安全，而多语言数据集训练能改善安全性能且不损害通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在全球部署，确保其在多语言环境中的安全性和对齐性变得至关重要。当前的安全行为在不同语言间存在不可预测的差异，这对一致且合乎伦理的AI构成了重大挑战。

Method: 作者引入了RefusEU数据集（覆盖12种欧洲语言），并采用受控的直接偏好优化（DPO）实验方法，系统研究多语言对齐的动态机制。

Result: 研究得出两个关键发现：1）仅在英语上进行对齐训练不足以确保跨语言安全（即使对于相同的危害类别）；2）在多语言数据集上训练可以提高安全性且不会降低通用性能（通过Global MMLU基准衡量）。

Conclusion: 为确保大语言模型的全球安全部署，需要专门的多语言对齐策略，仅依赖英语对齐是不够的。多语言训练为解决跨语言安全一致性问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are deployed globally, ensuring their safety and alignment across multiple languages becomes paramount. However, safety behaviors often vary unpredictably between languages, posing significant challenges for consistent and ethical AI. In this work, we systematically investigate the dynamics of multilingual alignment, exploring whether single-language alignment transfers cross-lingually, how language consistency is preserved during training, and the resulting trade-offs with general knowledge capabilities. We introduce RefusEU, a novel refusal alignment dataset covering 12 European languages, including a dedicated test set for evaluating current state-of-the-art models. Our controlled Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) experiments provide two key insights: aligning models exclusively in English is insufficient to ensure cross-lingual safety, even for the same harm categories, whereas training on multilingual datasets can improve safety without degrading general performance, as measured by the Global MMLU benchmark.

</details>


### [69] [From Architecture to Output: Structural Origins of Hallucination in Large Language Models and the Amplifying Role of Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07537)
*Md. Rejaul Korim Sadi,Toufiqur Rahman Tasin,Golam Mostofa Naeem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将大语言模型的幻觉问题归因于三个核心架构决策构成的复合故障系统：自注意力机制、最大似然估计训练目标和自回归解码机制，而非传统认为的数据集缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉分类框架（如Alansari和Luqman的分类法）虽然描述严谨，但仅按输出类型分类，无法识别产生特定幻觉实例的内部机制。论文旨在从架构层面分析幻觉的结构性根源。

Method: 通过理论分析将幻觉问题映射到三个核心架构决策：1）自注意力机制基于共现学习替代语义意义，导致实体混淆、事实归属错误和语义漂移；2）最大似然估计训练目标优化下一个词元概率而无事实约束；3）自回归解码在暴露偏差下的永久左向右承诺导致错误词元的级联传播。

Result: 论文建立了架构机制与输出类型分类的映射关系：内在幻觉源于自注意力机制，外在幻觉源于最大似然估计，逻辑不一致源于自回归解码。同时证明数据集缺陷（长尾不足、训练偏差、合成污染）只是放大这些机制漏洞而非独立原因。

Conclusion: 幻觉是三个架构决策共同形成的复合故障系统的结构性后果。仅基于输出类型的分类存在诊断局限性，需要转向推理层缓解方法，从架构层面解决幻觉的根本原因而非表面症状。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate--producing fluent, confident, factually wrong outputs--with a consistency that persists across generations and scales. Existing taxonomies classify hallucination by output type, distinguishing intrinsic from extrinsic failures and faithfulness from factuality divergence. These frameworks are descriptively rigorous but do not identify which internal mechanism produced a given instance. This paper analyses hallucination as a structural consequence of three architectural decisions that together form a compound failure system. Self-attention's co-occurrence learning substitutes statistical proximity for semantic meaning and produces entity confusion, fact misattribution, and semantic drift. The maximum likelihood estimation training objective optimises next-token probability without factual constraint, rewarding statistically plausible outputs regardless of their truth value. Autoregressive decoding's permanent left-to-right commitment under exposure bias ensures that a single wrong token cascades forward through the entire output sequence without revision. Dataset pathologies--long-tail deficiencies, training bias, and synthetic pollution--amplify these vulnerabilities but do not independently cause them. We make three contributions. First, we map each mechanism to a specific output category in the Alansari and Luqman taxonomy, locating intrinsic hallucination in self-attention, extrinsic hallucination in MLE, and logical inconsistency in autoregressive decoding. Second, we show that each commonly cited dataset pathology exploits one of these mechanisms rather than originating hallucination independently. Third, we identify the diagnostic limitation of output-type-only classification and contrast it with inference-layer mitigation approaches.

</details>


### [70] [Finding Hidden Relationships Between Medical Concepts by Leveraging Metamap and Text Mining Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07540)
*Weikang Yang,S M Mazharul Hoque Chowdhury,Wei Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种新模型，通过使用MetaMap和文本挖掘技术发现两个医学概念之间的隐藏关系。该模型创建了新的综合索引结构，能够找到大多数现有方法忽略的跨文档隐藏链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本是计算机世界中存储数据的最常见方式之一。虽然表面上看这些数据似乎互不关联，但实际上数据之间可能存在隐藏的联系。在医学领域，发现概念间的隐藏关系对于知识发现和临床决策具有重要意义。现有方法往往忽略了跨文档的隐藏链接，需要新的技术来解决这一问题。

Method: 研究提出了一种新模型，主要方法包括：1) 使用MetaMap进行医学概念识别和标准化；2) 应用适当的文本挖掘技术；3) 创建新的综合索引结构；4) 实现跨文档隐藏链接的发现机制。该模型能够识别并建立文档间被忽视的主题联系。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的模型在发现主题间新连接方面具有有效性。模型能够成功识别出医学概念之间的隐藏关系，证明了该方法在挖掘跨文档关联方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 本研究成功开发了一种能够发现医学概念间隐藏关系的新模型。通过结合MetaMap和文本挖掘技术，创建的综合索引结构有效地解决了跨文档隐藏链接的发现问题，为医学知识发现提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: Text is one of the most common ways to store data in this computerized world. At a glance, it may seem that those data are not interconnected. But in reality, data can have hidden connections. Therefore, in this research, a new model has been presented that can find hidden relationships between two medical concepts by using MetaMap and appropriate text-mining techniques. Specifically, the model creates a new comprehensive index structure and can find cross-document hidden links connecting topics of interest that most existing approaches have ignored. Experiments show the effectiveness of the proposed model in discovering new connections between topics.

</details>


### [71] [Liberating LLM Capabilities in Full-Duplex Speech Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07547)
*Luoyuan Zhang,Bokai Xu,Junbo Cui,Weiyue Sun,Yingjing Xu,Hanyu Liu,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Listen-Write-Speak (LWS)是一种文本优先的三通道范式，使语音大语言模型能够同时处理音频输入、产生可见文本输出和语音回复，支持代码生成、结构化分析等文本原生能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音大语言模型通常限制为语音回复，限制了用户输出仅为可语音化的内容，抑制了代码生成、结构化分析和多步推理等文本原生能力，且缺乏持久、结构化、可检查的中间输出。

Method: 提出Listen-Write-Speak (LWS)文本优先三通道范式，通过单一的自动回归LLM连续监听用户音频，将可见自由格式文本作为主要输出，并在共享因果注意力上下文中并行生成实时口头回复。该行为通过Token Schema实现，无需架构修改，采用两阶段数据流水线学习，合成与揭示输入时间线一致的每秒认知注释。

Result: LWS在Full-Duplex-Bench上表现出强大的全双工交互能力，在VoiceBench AlpacaEval上达到4.72分，书写-语音一致性达92.6%，在URO-Bench上始终优于其内部消融实验。

Conclusion: 可见书写可以作为语音交互的一等输出通道，而不会牺牲实时响应性，为语音大语言模型提供了文本原生能力的支持。

Abstract: Speech-based large language models are typically constrained to spoken replies, which limits their user-facing outputs to what can be verbalized and suppresses text-native capabilities such as code generation, structured analysis, and multi-step reasoning in realtime interaction, for tasks that require persistent, structured, and inspectable intermediate outputs. Existing work improves spoken reasoning or full-duplex turn-taking, but still treats text as a hidden intermediate state or a subordinate modality rather than a first-class output channel. We propose Listen-Write-Speak (LWS), a text-first tri-channel paradigm in which a single autoregressive LLM continuously listens to user audio, writes visible free-form text as its primary output, and speaks a realtime oral response in parallel under a shared causal attention context. This behavior is implemented entirely through a Token Schema, requiring no architectural modifications, and learned via a two-stage data pipeline that synthesizes per-second cognitive annotations consistent with the revealed input timeline. Empirically, LWS demonstrates strong full-duplex interaction on Full-Duplex-Bench, reaches 4.72 on VoiceBench AlpacaEval, achieves 92.6% writing-speaking consistency, and consistently outperforms its internal ablations on URO-Bench. These results suggest that visible writing can serve as a first-class output channel for speech interaction without sacrificing realtime responsiveness. The code and dataset are available on the project page: https://royalzhang.com/project/lws-page/.

</details>


### [72] [Priors Persist Through Suppression: A Stroop Paradigm for Lexical Override](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07555)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过斯特鲁普范式实验发现，语言模型在适应新词义映射时，词汇先验知识持续存在并产生干扰，而非被完全覆盖；机制分析揭示了词汇先验是干扰的源头和改写操作的标记位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于理解语言模型如何处理词汇重新映射（如技术术语表、系统提示中的特殊用法），特别关注当模型被要求以不熟悉的方式使用熟悉词汇时，其内在的词汇先验知识是会被新含义完全替代，还是持续存在并产生干扰。

Method: 采用斯特鲁普式实验范式：建立重映射规则（如"医生"表示"森林"），与查询词的词汇先验干扰项（如"医院"）形成竞争，设置匹配的中性对照。在11个开源模型（涵盖4个模型家族，参数1B-9B）上进行实验，控制项目级别的答案先验、频率、分词和提示措辞。对5个对齐模型进行激活修补，定位定义主语、定义目标、查询词的三元组位置。

Result: 词汇先验强度即使在控制了多个变量后仍能预测干扰效应。激活修补发现特定三元组位置几乎完全恢复了冲突效应（聚合R∈[0.92, 1.06]）。定义目标交换实验表明三元组执行的是绑定操作而非身份匹配。解离实验显示目标保留是绑定特异性特征：干扰抑制在所有条件下均发生，而目标logit崩溃仅在定义目标位置被破坏时出现。

Conclusion: 行为和机制分析均指向同一通道：词汇先验既是干扰的起源，也是改写操作留下标记的位置。语言模型在处理词汇重新映射时，词汇先验知识通过压制而非替换的方式持续存在，这解释了为何模型在学习新用法时仍然受到原有词义的影响。

Abstract: Glossaries, technical specifications, and system prompts routinely ask language models to use familiar words in unfamiliar ways. When this works, the lexical prior persists through override rather than being replaced: it continues to operate after the local rule applies, with the rule lowering its logit rather than installing the new meaning on top. We test this with a Stroop-style paradigm: a remapping rule ("doctor" means "forest") pitted against the query word's lexical-prior distractor ("hospital"), with matched neutral controls. Across 11 open-weight models spanning four families and 1B--9B parameters, lexical-prior strength predicts interference even after item-level controls for answer prior, frequency, tokenization, and prompt wording. Activation patching on five aligned models locates a source-position triplet (definition subject, definition target, query word) that nearly fully recovers the conflict effect (aggregate $R \in [0.92, 1.06]$). A definition-target swap shows the triplet performs binding rather than identity matching. Dissociation experiments isolate target preservation as the binding-specific signature: distractor suppression occurs under matched, swap, and item-mismatched conditions alike, whereas target logit collapse occurs only when the definition-target position is corrupted. Behavior and mechanism converge on the same channel: the lexical prior is where both interference originates and where override leaves its mark.

</details>


### [73] [Phantom transitions in language model fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07559)
*Vaibhav Prakash,Jayasri Dontabhaktuni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，在近义词竞争语境中微调语言模型会静默失败：损失下降但正确标记的排名从未超过竞争对手。作者通过秩序参数分解失败模式，观察到类似相变的跳跃现象，但证明这些过渡是"幻影"，完全由softmax读出导致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在包含近义词竞争语境微调时的静默失败现象。虽然交叉熵损失单调下降，但正确标记的排名从未超过其竞争对手，这种失败模式在传统评估中难以察觉。

Method: 使用5种不同架构的Transformer模型（覆盖两个家族和5倍参数范围），在10个手工选择的近义词语境上进行实验。定义了一个结合预测分布和嵌入重叠的秩序参数，将其可加性分解为信号（追踪正确标记相对于最近竞争者的优势）和背景拖累（由嵌入体向分数泄漏概率的程度决定）。对比了完全微调和LoRA微调。

Result: 发现两种失败模式：运动学失败（信号保持很小）和结构失败（拖累随微调恶化）。观察到类似相变的跳跃现象，但证明这些过渡是"幻影"而非真正的对称破缺相变，因为LoRA微调（令牌嵌入矩阵不变）中也出现跳跃。少量无量纲量能组织不同架构的轨迹，其中一个在所有完全微调模型中一致，第二个按嵌入分布将架构分为两类并预测LoRA适用性。

Conclusion: 跳跃不连续性完全存在于softmax读出中，而非嵌入几何相变。该框架能准确预测未使用架构的临界学习率（误差2.1%），但研究发现仅适用于近义词机制，不能未经重新校准就外推到其他情况。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on contexts whose correct completion has a near-synonym competitor often fails silently. The cross-entropy loss decreases monotonically while the correct token never overtakes the competitor in rank. We study this regime across five transformer architectures spanning two families and a fivefold parameter range, on ten hand-selected near-synonym contexts. We instrument these failures with an order parameter combining the predicted distribution and pairwise embedding overlaps. It decomposes additively into a signal, tracking the model's commitment to the correct token over its nearest competitor, and a background drag, set by how the embedding bulk leaks probability into the score. This isolates two failure modes. In kinematic failure the signal stays small. In structural failure the drag actively worsens as fine-tuning proceeds. We observe sharp catapult-like jumps in the order parameter that resemble a phase transition. A central negative result organises the paper. The transitions are phantoms. The spontaneous-symmetry-breaking interpretation is ruled out by direct measurement. Catapult-like jumps still appear under LoRA fine-tuning with the token embedding matrix exactly unchanged during training, where no geometric phase transition is possible. The discontinuity lives entirely in the softmax readout. A small number of dimensionless quantities organise the trajectory across architectures. One is consistent across all five under full fine-tuning. A second sorts architectures into two classes by bulk embedding distribution and predicts LoRA sufficiency. As a blind test, the framework predicts the critical learning rate of a held-out architecture, not used to fit any parameter, to within 2.1% of a subsequent learning-rate sweep. Findings concern the near-synonym mechanism only and should not be extrapolated without recalibration.

</details>


### [74] [Function-Vector Heads Are Two Populations: Writers and Cancellers in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07560)
*Han-yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了函数向量头（FV heads）实际上包含两个功能相反的子群体：写入器（writers）和消除器（cancellers），它们分别提升和降低规则正确概率的对数。这一结构在仅基于幅度排序的方法中是不可见的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Todd等人（2024）识别函数向量头时，通常基于它们对上下文规则任务的因果贡献幅度，隐含假设这些头部是单一功能类别。这种假设是错误的，需要更精细的方法来理解FV头的真实功能结构。

Method: 使用符号保持标准（精炼的DLA + 置换FDR）替换仅基于幅度的排序，并通过路径修补验证每个候选头。采用四条件典型判决和符号混洗来检验同质性假设。

Result: 在三个模型家族和六个Pythia规模的13/15个单元中验证了四条件典型判决；符号混洗在5/6个主要单元中拒绝了同质性假设。零消除消除器导致对数增益增加0.13到0.29纳特，准确率提升2-7个百分点。

Conclusion: 函数向量头不是单一功能类别，而是包含功能相反的写入器和消除器两个子群体。这种二分结构对理解语言模型的内部计算机制至关重要，且能显著改善模型性能。

Abstract: Function-vector (FV) heads (Todd et al., 2024) are typically identified by the magnitude of their causal contribution to in-context rule tasks, under the implicit assumption that the top set is a homogeneous functional class. This assumption fails. We replace magnitude-only ranking with a sign-preserving criterion (refined DLA + permutation FDR) and validate each candidate by path patching. The FV head population then splits into two opposing sub-populations: writers push the rule-correct logit up; cancellers push it down. A four-condition canonical verdict holds in $13/15$ cells across three model families and six Pythia scales, and a sign-shuffle rejects homogeneity in $5/6$ main cells. The structure is invisible to magnitude-only ranking: Todd's top-$20$ captures $64\%$ of cancellers but only $4\%$ of writers on the hierarchical task, and $59\%$ of writers but only $8\%$ of cancellers on the modular task. We rule out six artefact accounts on all $27$ canceller (cell, head) pairs: induction overlap, sinks, generic importance, rank-$1$ copy-suppression, V-cascade, and rank-nearest non-FV controls.
