Zero-ablating cancellers yields $+0.13$ to $+0.29$ nats of logit gain in $6/6$ main cells with a directionally consistent $+2$ to $+7$ pp accuracy effect.

</details>


### [75] [Subtitle-Aligned Fine-Tuning of Whisper for Swiss German ASR: Benchmark Contamination, Convention Mismatch, and an Honest Baseline at 25.6% WER (13.8% cWER)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07608)
*Felix Akeret*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了在瑞士德语ASR任务上微调Whisper large-v3的性能，使用1,367小时广播语音与德语字幕作为弱监督数据。通过16次迭代实验比较LoRA与全微调策略，分析幻觉成因，并量化数据质量、字幕对齐和训练策略的影响。研究发现现有SOTA结果存在基准污染问题，并发布两个公开模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于：1）评估Whisper在瑞士德语（低资源方言）ASR任务上的微调效果；2）系统分析训练策略、数据质量等因素对性能的影响；3）揭示现有瑞士德语ASR基准可能存在的污染问题，即模型可能通过记忆测试集而非真正理解方言来获得高评分；4）提供公开可复现的瑞士德语ASR模型。

Method: 研究方法包括：1）使用1,367小时广播语音与德语字幕作为弱监督数据进行训练；2）在NVIDIA DGX Spark（Grace Blackwell架构）上进行16次迭代实验；3）比较LoRA（参数高效微调）与全微调策略；4）分析幻觉问题的根本原因；5）量化数据质量、字幕对齐和训练策略的影响；6）使用Phi-4-multimodal模型验证基准污染效应；7）进行误差分析，区分真实识别错误与有效的风格变异（时态、词序、瑞士拼写）。

Result: 主要结果：1）最佳模型在ASGDTS测试集上获得25.6% WER（严格不重叠数据评估）；2）经过误差分离后，内容WER（cWER）为13.8%，仅计算实际识别失败；3）偏差校正估计进一步将cWER降至8.5%；4）发现现有SOTA结果（17.1-17.5% WER）存在基准污染：使用零瑞士德语数据的Whisper模型在ASGDTS测试集上自训练后达到13.88% WER，超过所有已发布系统；5）Phi-4-multimodal实验显示更强的记忆效应（3.9% WER），表明基准主要衡量匹配度而非方言理解能力。

Conclusion: 研究结论：1）通过系统微调和误差分析，可以显著提升瑞士德语ASR性能；2）现有瑞士德语ASR基准存在严重污染问题，导致已发表结果被高估；3）基准可能主要评估模型对测试集约定的匹配能力，而非真正的方言理解；4）发布了两个公开模型（LoRA适配器和全微调模型），为瑞士德语ASR研究提供可靠基准；5）强调在低资源方言ASR评估中需要使用严格不重叠数据并进行细致的误差分析。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of fine-tuning OpenAI's Whisper large-v3 for Swiss German ASR, using 1,367 hours of broadcast speech paired with Standard German subtitles as weak supervision. Through 16 iterative training runs on an NVIDIA DGX Spark (Grace Blackwell, 128 GB unified memory, up to 1 PFLOP FP4), we compare LoRA and full fine-tuning of the 1.55B-parameter model, investigate hallucination root causes, and quantify the effect of data quality, subtitle alignment, and training strategy. Our best model achieves 25.6% measured WER on the All Swiss German Dialects Test Set (ASGDTS) in an honest evaluation on strictly disjoint data. A harmonized error analysis separating genuine errors from valid stylistic variation (tense, word order, Swiss orthography) yields a content WER (cWER) of 13.8%, counting only actual recognition failures. Bias-corrected estimation reduces this to 8.5%, suggesting the true error rate is roughly one third of measured WER.
  We demonstrate that published state-of-the-art Swiss German ASR results (17.1-17.5% WER) are inflated by benchmark contamination: a vanilla Whisper model self-trained on the ASGDTS test set with zero Swiss German data achieves 13.88% WER, surpassing all published systems. Experiments with Phi-4-multimodal show an even stronger memorization effect (3.9% WER), revealing that the benchmark primarily measures convention matching rather than dialectal comprehension.
  We release two models, a LoRA adapter (25.32% WER, 13.9% cWER) and a full fine-tuned model (25.60% WER, 13.8% cWER), among the few publicly available, honestly evaluated Whisper models for Swiss German, under Apache 2.0 with full reproducibility, requiring no institutional data agreements.

</details>


### [76] [ReadingMachine: A Computational Methodology for Structured Corpus Reading and Large-Scale Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07753)
*James Morrissey*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReadingMachine是一种基于大型语言模型的语料库结构化阅读方法，通过可检查的分析阶段处理整个文档集合，优先保证覆盖度、可追溯性和保留分歧，在152份产业政策文档上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的语料库分析方法如检索或递归摘要往往导致不可逆的压缩，损失原始信息，缺乏可追溯性，且难以保留不同观点间的分歧。需要一种能够处理大规模异构文档集合，同时保持分析透明度和覆盖度的方法。

Method: 1. 将分析分解为可检查的阶段：洞察提取、语义聚类、主题生成、迭代遗漏检测
2. 延迟不可逆的压缩过程
3. 明确追踪中间表示
4. 使用大型语言模型执行有界阅读操作
5. 避免依赖检索或递归摘要

Result: 1. 在152份异构的产业政策文档上进行了演示
2. 提取了超过17,500个洞察
3. 生成了结构化的主题地图
4. 展示了该方法在大规模语料分析中的有效性
5. 系统已作为开源实验框架发布

Conclusion: ReadingMachine提供了一种可扩展、可追溯的语料库分析方法，特别适用于大规模定性综合和语料分析，通过结构化的工作流程和中间表示的明确追踪，在保持覆盖度的同时增强了分析的可解释性。

Abstract: ReadingMachine is a computational methodology for structured corpus reading that uses large language models to perform bounded reading operations over entire document collections. Rather than relying on retrieval or recursive summarization, the approach decomposes analysis into inspectable stages including insight extraction, semantic clustering, theme generation, and iterative omission detection. By delaying irreversible compression and explicitly tracking intermediate representations, the method prioritizes coverage, traceability, and preservation of disagreement across large corpora. The system is demonstrated on a heterogeneous corpus of 152 industrial policy documents, producing more than 17,500 extracted insights and a structured thematic map. ReadingMachine is released as an open-source experimental framework for large-scale qualitative synthesis and corpus analysis.

</details>


### [77] [Unlocking Latent Value: Taxonomy-Guided Recovery of High-Performing Data from Low-Tier Web Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07778)
*Neeraj Varshney,Sanket Lokegaonkar,Nasser Zalmout,Qingyu Yin,Priyanka Nigam,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于分类学的多维过滤框架，通过语义维度而非单一综合评分来挖掘被降级网络数据中的潜在价值，显著提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络数据预处理流水线通常将文档质量压缩为单一综合评分，这种评分会系统性低估某些高价值内容，导致大量有价值数据被降级处理。

Method: 1. 基于ESSENTIAL-WEB分类学引入时效性和文化特异性两个新维度，使用Qwen2.5 32B标注1400万文档并蒸馏到0.5B轻量模型；2. 训练7300万参数的多任务MLP在E5嵌入上实现50倍推理吞吐；3. 提出计算高效的两阶段框架：第一阶段在小规模识别强维度信号，第二阶段从最佳维度构建并评估合取和析取复合过滤器。

Result: 分类学过滤的子集优于未过滤基线，甚至超越最高质量层级。在中层数据上，最佳过滤器相比未过滤基线在推理、编码和知识基准上分别提升12.1%、9.5%和2.0%，在推理和编码上分别超过未过滤顶级数据6.7%和13.7%。从低于生产阈值两个层级的数据中，过滤后数据在推理和编码上分别提升22.3%和19.5%，在编码基准上超越顶级数据。

Conclusion: 被降级的网络数据中仍存在大量潜在价值，多维分类学过滤是计算高效、原则性的解锁方法，能够显著提升下游任务性能。

Abstract: Dominant web data curation pipelines for pretraining collapse document quality into a single composite score, systematically missing high-value content along dimensions the scorer underweights. We present a taxonomy-driven framework that recovers this value by filtering along semantically meaningful dimensions that composite scores fail to capture. First, building on the ESSENTIAL-WEB taxonomy, we introduce two novel dimensions: timeliness and cultural specificity, both of which show low pairwise NMI with existing ones. We annotate 14M documents using Qwen2.5 32B and distill into a lightweight 0.5B model. To enable rapid corpus-wide annotation, we additionally train a 73M multi-task MLP on E5 embeddings, achieving 50x inference throughput. Second, to navigate the combinatorial explosion of filter configurations, we introduce a compute-efficient two-pass framework: Pass 1 identifies the strongest dimension signals at small scale; Pass 2 constructs and evaluates conjunctive and disjunctive compound filters from the top performers - identifying high-performing configurations at a fraction of full scaling-law cost. Applying the selected filters to deprioritized web data, taxonomy-filtered subsets outperform their unfiltered baselines and even surpass the highest-quality tier. On mid-tier data, our best filter improves over its unfiltered baseline by 12.1% on reasoning, 9.5% on coding, and 2.0% on knowledge benchmarks, exceeding unfiltered top-tier data by 6.7% on reasoning and 13.7% on coding. Furthermore, filtered data from two tiers below the typical production threshold improves by 22.3% on reasoning and 19.5% on coding over its unfiltered baseline, surpassing top-tier data on coding benchmarks. These results establish that vast latent value remains locked in deprioritized web data, and that multi-dimensional taxonomy filtering is a principled, compute-efficient key to unlocking it.

</details>


### [78] [Evaluating RAG Reliability under Clean, Misleading, and Mixed Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07783)
*Sevgi Yigit-Sert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种评估协议，用于系统测试RAG系统如何处理参数知识与不同误导信息量的检索证据之间的冲突，通过结合参数覆盖和置信度指标来分析误导信息对LLM生成过程的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然检索增强生成(RAG)广泛用于提高大语言模型的事实可靠性，但在包含大量错误信息的环境中，检索内容可能包含看似合理但错误的信息，这引发了人们对RAG系统在信息访问中可靠性的担忧。

Method: 提出一个评估协议，针对LLM在无检索时也能正确回答的事实性问题，测试系统在不同证据条件下的表现：1）清洁证据，2）中毒证据，3）混合证据。分析方法结合参数覆盖和置信度指标来评估误导信息如何影响LLM的生成过程。

Result: 研究通过系统化的测试协议，分析RAG系统在面对参数知识与检索证据冲突时的行为模式，特别关注误导信息对生成过程的具体影响机制。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在为RAG系统在信息混乱场景中的鲁棒性提供深入见解，帮助理解和改进RAG系统在面对误导信息时的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is widely used to improve the factual reliability of large language models (LLMs) by grounding answers in retrieved evidence. In misinformation-rich environments, however, retrieved content may include plausible but incorrect information, raising concerns about the reliability of RAG-based information access systems. In this work, we propose an evaluation protocol to systematically test how the RAG system handles conflicts between parametric knowledge and evidence retrieved from context with varying amounts of misleading information. We target correct answers to factoid questions that the model responds to correctly, even when there is no retrieval, and use this to test the system with clean, poisoned, and mixed evidence. The proposed analytical framework combines parametric override and confidence metrics to assess when and how misleading information affects the generation process of LLMs. This study aims to provide insights into the robustness of RAG systems in information disorder scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [SLMJury: Can Small Language Models Judge as Well as Large Ones?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07810)
*Anish Laddha,Nitesh Pradhan,Gaurav Srivastava*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SLMJury框架评估小型语言模型作为评审员，研究发现评审性能与模型规模、领域特性和评审范式密切相关，无需依赖大型专有模型即可实现可靠自动评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为评估工具成本高、延迟大且不透明，限制了可扩展性，因此需要研究小型语言模型作为替代评审员的可行性和性能。

Method: 提出SLMJury框架，在16个小语言模型（0.6B-14B参数）上评估两种评审范式：封闭式二元正确性判定和开放式质量评分，涵盖十个基准测试，包括八个封闭式任务和两个开放式任务，并研究了过度思考效应、领域泛化等五个维度。

Result: 发现过度思考效应具有领域依赖性，数学任务中短回答表现更好；领域泛化能力因模型系列差异显著；封闭式和开放式评审需要不同能力；多智能体辩论协议会降低准确性，但顶级评审员能抵抗对抗性角色干扰。

Conclusion: 可靠的自动评估不需要大型专有模型，但没有单个小型语言模型在所有领域都表现最优，不同评审任务需要选择最适合的模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used as judges for evaluating model outputs, but their high cost, latency, and opacity limit scalability. We introduce SLMJury, a framework for evaluating small language models (SLMs) as judges across two paradigms: closed-ended binary correctness and open-ended quality scoring. We benchmark 16 SLM judges (0.6B-14B parameters) from four model families across ten benchmarks: eight closed-ended tasks spanning mathematical, scientific, and general reasoning (N=64,824 judgments per configuration), plus SummEval and MT-Bench for summarization and conversational scoring. We formalize judging as a budget-conditioned function and study five dimensions. Four findings emerge. (1) The overthinking effect is domain-dependent: for most judges quick 10-token verdicts match or beat extended reasoning on mathematical judging (by 2-7% where they help), while reasoning wins on general tasks by up to 23%. (2) Domain generalization separates model families, with math-to-general accuracy gaps ranging from under 10% to nearly 40%. (3) Closed-ended and open-ended judging draw on different capabilities: the best binary judge (Phi-4) drops to rank 9 on MT-Bench, while reasoning-trained models invert this ordering. (4) Under the Reflect-Critique-Refine (RCR) debate protocol, multi-agent debate degrades accuracy across all tested configurations, whereas the top judges resist six adversarial personas with <=0.55% variance. Reliable automated evaluation does not require large proprietary models, yet no single SLM dominates. The leaderboard is available at https://anishh15.github.io/SLMJury/, and our framework code and pip package are publicly available at https://github.com/anishh15/SLMJury and https://pypi.org/project/slmjury/.

</details>


### [80] [Representational Similarity and Model Behavior in Multi-Agent Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07818)
*Yujin Potter,Seun Eisape,Shiyang Lai,Alexander Huth,James Evans,Been Kim,Jacob Eisenstein,Dawn Song,Alane Suhr*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究表明，人工智能模型间的表征相似性可以预测合作成功率，但会降低新颖性和创造力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究神经相似性对人类社交亲密度和合作成功的预测作用是否也适用于人工智能系统，特别是大型语言模型之间的交互。

Method: 通过276对模型在8个游戏任务中的交互实验，涵盖合作和新颖性任务，分析表征相似性与合作/创新表现的关系。

Result: 表征相似性较高的模型对在合作任务中表现更好，但在新颖性和创造力方面表现较差；早期层的相似性与合作和新颖性关系最强。

Conclusion: 表征相似性是影响多智能体系统设计的重要因素，共享的词汇和语义基础可能是这一模式的关键机制。

Abstract: Researchers have shown that neural similarity among humans predicts social closeness and cooperative success, whereas innovation often emerges from interactions among dissimilar individuals. We investigate whether these principles extend to artificial intelligence by examining interactions between large language models. In our experiments, 276 model pairs interact across eight games spanning both cooperation and novelty. We find that pairs with more similar representation spaces achieve significantly higher cooperation but exhibit reduced novelty and creativity. The effects of representational similarity on cooperation and novelty remain robust even after controlling for other factors such as performance disparity and model size. We also find that similarity in the early layers consistently shows the strongest association with cooperation and novelty, compared to the middle and later layers. This suggests that a central factor underlying these patterns could be the extent to which the two models share lexical and semantic grounding. Overall, representational similarity can be an important consideration in multi-agent system design.

</details>


### [81] [The ACUTE Protocol: Operationalizing Language Model Activations for Better Calibration, Utility, and Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07822)
*Nishant Subramani,Palash Goyal,Yiwen Song,Mani Malek,Yuan Xue,Tomas Pfister,Hamid Palangi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EURO新指标平衡校准性与信息性，并开发ACUTE协议实现灵活高效的置信度估计，提升语言模型的校准性、效用性和可信度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语言模型部署到各种任务中，可信度变得至关重要。校准性作为可信度的代理指标很重要，但现有模型即使性能提升，校准性仍然很差（常偏向过度自信），且校准性可以被操纵（如总是预测基准率虽完美校准但无信息量）。需要同时平衡校准性和信息性。

Method: 1. 开发EURO（expected utility renormalized by the oracle）新指标，同时衡量校准性和信息性；2. 提出ACUTE（activation-based confidence, utility, and trust estimation）协议，基于激活的通用置信度、效用和信任评估协议，提供灵活、样本高效、计算高效的置信度估计器。

Result: ACUTE协议在多项选择问答、工具调用和科学文档摘要三个任务上，跨越4个模型家族的6个模型进行了验证。ACUTE在EURO指标上优于强基线，同时保持较低的校准误差。该协议改进了语言模型的校准性、效用性和可信度。

Conclusion: 通过EURO指标和ACUTE协议的结合，可以显著提升语言模型的校准性、效用性和可信度。该工作展示了为LLM配备ACUTE协议能够在多种场景中改善模型的置信度估计，平衡校准性与信息性之间的权衡。

Abstract: As language models improve and become increasingly deployed to solve a variety of tasks, trustworthiness becomes essential. Calibration is a good proxy for trust: well-calibrated confidence estimates help inform the risk versus reward tradeoff when trusting a specific model output. Unfortunately, even as models improve, they remain poorly calibrated, often biasing towards overconfidence. Additionally, calibration can be gamed: a policy that always predicts the base rate is perfectly calibrated, but completely uninformative. To resolve this, we develop a new metric, expected utility renormalized by the oracle (EURO), that balances calibration and informativeness. We also propose a general-purpose activation-based confidence, utility, and trust estimation protocol (ACUTE) to appropriately adjudicate uncertainty. The ACUTE protocol provides flexible, sample-efficient, and compute-efficient confidence estimators for 3 tasks including multiple choice question answering, tool-calling, and scientific document summarization across 6 models from 4 model families. ACUTE outperforms strong baselines on EURO, while maintaining low calibration error. Taken together, our work shows that equipping LLMs with the ACUTE protocol can improve calibration, utility, and trustworthiness in numerous settings.

</details>


### [82] [Beyond English benchmarks: clinical llm evaluation in Brazilian Portuguese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07853)
*Giordano de Pinho Souza,Glaucia Melo,Josefino Cabral Melo Lima,Daniel Schneider*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ClinicalBr，这是首个基于真实巴西病例报告构建的双语临床决策基准，用于评估大语言模型在葡萄牙语和英语上的表现，发现语言性能差异是任务依赖性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数临床决策基准都是在英语环境下进行的，需要进行跨语言评估以解决全球医疗访问中的语言障碍问题，特别是在巴西等葡萄牙语使用地区。

Method: 构建了ClinicalBr双语基准，包含从28个SciELO医学期刊中提取的2,892个真实巴西病例报告，涵盖18个专科，并构建为葡萄牙语-英语平行语料。每个病例支持四个评估任务：诊断检索、鉴别诊断、检查推荐和治疗计划。评估了MedGemma-27B、Sabiá-4、DeepSeek-R1和o3-mini四个模型在两种语言上的表现。

Result: 葡萄牙语-英语性能差异是任务依赖性的，而非普遍存在：1）在诊断检索任务中，英语在所有模型中都有持续优势（+7.5-12.1准确率点）；2）在鉴别诊断、检查推荐和治疗计划任务中，这种优势消失，大多数模型的置信区间跨越零，且葡萄牙语的完整性分数略高；3）巴西特有疾病比完整语料库更容易处理，表明热带病表现在当前预训练中得到充分体现；4）检查推荐是所有模型和两种语言中最困难的任务（F1分数<0.10），远低于鉴别诊断的上限（0.20-0.27）。

Conclusion: 语言差距对临床决策的影响是任务特异性的，而不是普遍存在的。巴西特有疾病没有给模型带来额外困难，这表明当前语言模型的预训练已充分覆盖热带疾病表现。检查推荐任务的低性能揭示了临床决策支持系统需要改进的关键领域。

Abstract: Large Language Models are transforming the support for clinical decision and their application in real scenarios. Yet, most benchmarks are conducted in English, and cross-lingual evaluation is needed to tackle the language gaps in global access. We introduce ClinicalBr, the first bilingual benchmark for clinical decision built from real Brazilian case reports. The corpus contains 2,892 cases drawn from 28 SciELO medical journals, spanning 18 specialties, and is structured as parallel Portuguese-English pairs. Each case supports four evaluation tasks: diagnosis retrieval, differential diagnosis, exam recommendation, and treatment planning. We evaluate four models: MedGemma-27B, Sabiá-4, DeepSeek-R1, and o3-mini, across both languages. The central finding is that the Portuguese-English performance gap is task-dependent, not general. In diagnosis retrieval, English yields a consistent advantage across all models, with +7.5-12.1 accuracy points. This advantage disappears in differential diagnosis, exam recommendation, and treatment planning, where confidence intervals cross zero for most models and Portuguese completeness scores are marginally higher. Brazilian-endemic conditions proved easier than the full corpus, not harder, indicating that tropical presentations are adequately represented in current pre-training. Exam recommendation was the hardest task across all models and both languages, with F1 scores below 0.10, well below the differential diagnosis ceiling of 0.20-0.27.

</details>


### [83] [The Cold-Start Safety Gap in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07867)
*Chung-En Sun,Linbo Liu,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现工具调用LLM代理在对话开始时安全性最脆弱，随着完成正常代理任务数量增加会变得更安全，这种现象被称为"冷启动安全差距"。作者提出了SODA基准测试，发现完成20个前置任务后安全性提升9-52%，并建议在部署前让代理先完成几个正常任务来缓解安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM单轮对话的安全性，但缺乏对多轮对话中工具调用代理安全性的系统研究。作者发现代理在会话开始时存在安全隐患，需要探究这种冷启动安全现象的本质和解决方案。

Method: 1. 提出SODA基准测试，控制代理在遇到安全威胁前完成的正向代理任务数量（最多20个）；2. 评估7个模型家族的模型；3. 进行表示分析研究隐藏状态的变化；4. 分析对话中哪些部分对安全性影响最大；5. 在开源安全基准（AgentHarm、Agent Safety Bench）和效用基准（BFCL、API-Bank）上进行验证。

Result: 1. 安全性随前置任务数量增加而显著提升（9-52%）；2. 隐藏状态逐渐向安全对齐区域移动；3. 正常代理任务是安全性提升的主要驱动因素，而代理自身的先前响应对安全性影响较小但对保持后续效用至关重要；4. 预热策略既能提高安全性又能保持完整能力。

Conclusion: 工具调用LLM代理存在冷启动安全差距，在部署前让代理完成几个正常代理任务可以有效缓解安全风险。这是一个简单实用的部署策略，能同时保证安全性和效用。

Abstract: Are tool-calling LLM agents equally safe throughout a conversation? We discover they are not: agents are most vulnerable at the very start of a session and become substantially safer after a few regular agentic tasks -- a phenomenon we term the cold-start safety gap. To study this systematically, we introduce Safety Over Depth for Agents (SODA), a benchmark that controls how many regular agentic tasks the agent completes before encountering a safety threat, supporting up to 20 preceding tasks. Evaluating 7 models from 4 families, safety improves by 9--52% as the number of preceding regular agentic tasks increases from zero to twenty. Representation analysis confirms that model hidden states gradually shift toward a safety-aligned region as more preceding tasks are present. By systematically studying which part of the preceding conversation matters most, we find that the regular agentic tasks themselves are the primary driver of safety, while the agent's own prior responses have less effect on safety but are essential for preserving later utility. This conclusion is further supported by evaluation on open-source safety benchmarks (AgentHarm, Agent Safety Bench) and utility benchmarks (BFCL, API-Bank), confirming that warming up the agent with regular agentic tasks before deployment makes it safer and preserves full capability. Based on these findings, we recommend a simple deployment strategy: having the agent complete a few regular agentic tasks before possible exposure to safety-critical requests mitigates the cold-start safety gap. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trustworthy-ML-Lab/Agent-Cold-Start-Safety-Gap

</details>


### [84] [Whose Norms? Disentangling Cultural and Personal Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07877)
*Angana Borah,Isabelle Augenstein,Rada Mihalcea*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PACT框架评估LLMs在文化规范与个人偏好之间的权衡选择，发现模型文化遵循程度受国家背景影响最大，且人类文化遵循存在内部多样性，模型只能匹配多数选择但无法捕捉文化多元性和不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将文化对齐和个性化分开探讨，而现实社交决策需要平衡文化规范与个人偏好（如诚实偏好与文化中间接反馈规范），缺乏统一的评估框架来研究这种权衡。

Method: 提出了PACT（个人偏好与文化规范权衡）框架，用于评估模型选择遵循文化规范还是允许个人偏好；进行了多国人类研究（五国）和人类-LLM对齐实验；分析了国家背景、年龄、性别对模型行为的影响及指令调优后的非均匀变化。

Result: 1. LLMs在文化规范遵循程度存在差异，国家背景影响最大（7.8%），年龄（1%）和性别（0.7%）影响较小；指令调优后行为非均匀变化。2. 人类文化遵循主要受情景国家驱动，对自己文化背景下判断时一致性最低，显示文化内部多元性。3. 模型只能匹配多数选择但无法捕捉响应分布和不确定性（最佳相关系数仅0.24）。

Conclusion: 研究结果推动对齐评估需要超越多数选择，能够捕捉社会判断中的文化多元性和分歧，以更好地理解模型在复杂社交决策中的行为。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for social decision-making situations that require balancing cultural norms with personal preferences. For example, a user preferring honesty might ask whether to correct a coworker publicly when local norms favor indirect feedback. Yet existing research studies cultural alignment and personalization largely separately. We introduce PACT, the Personal-Preference and Cultural-Norm Trade-off framework, which evaluates whether models choose to follow a cultural norm or allow personal preferences. We find that LLMs vary in how rigidly they enforce cultural norms, with behavior shifted more by country context (7.8%) than age (1%) and gender (0.7%) and shifting non-uniformly after instruction tuning. Furthermore, our five-country human study on PACT shows that culture-following in humans is mainly driven by scenario country, with the lowest agreement when participants judge their own cultural contexts, showing within-culture pluralism. Finally, human-LLM alignment experiments show that models can match majority choices, but fail to capture response distributions and uncertainty (with best correlations reaching only 0.24). Together, these findings motivate alignment evaluations that go beyond majority to capture cultural pluralism and disagreement in social judgment.

</details>


### [85] [Beyond Individual Personas: Aligning Synthetic Dialogue to Population-Level Behavior Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07893)
*Xinyi Liu,Rinat Khaziev,Hooshang Nayyeri,Emine Yilmaz,Charith Peris,Hari Thadakamalla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GroupPersona框架通过将群体行为统计转化为生成控制，使合成对话语料库与参考语料库的行为分布对齐，显著改善合成对话的群体级行为混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于角色的对话生成器优化单个对话的局部合理性，但会导致合成语料库在群体层面出现行为分布扭曲，无法准确反映参考人群的交互模式。

Method: GroupPersona框架将群体统计转化为生成控制：分离对话的核心行为特征与可预测的副作用，利用行为组来调节用户代理，使其遵循参考人群的交互模式。在两种对话来源（助手风格和Reddit衍生）和两种构建变体（结构保持和变化增强）的四个语料库上进行评估。

Result: 在12个行为属性上，GroupPersona将合成与参考分布之间的Jensen-Shannon散度从0.234降低到0.177（相对于最强基线减少24.4%），在四个语料库上均表现最佳或并列最佳，同时保持结构对齐。在对话质量分数校准方面，将平均绝对偏差从0.91（次优基线）降低到0.63，最接近参考对话质量分数。

Conclusion: GroupPersona能够有效对齐合成对话语料库与参考语料库的行为分布，解决现有方法导致的群体级行为混合扭曲问题，为生成更真实、更具代表性的合成对话数据提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Synthetic dialogue corpora are increasingly used as proxies for target dialogue data, yet persona-grounded generators optimize individual conversations rather than corpus composition, yielding locally plausible dialogues with distorted population-level behavior mixes. We introduce GroupPersona, a framework that aligns synthetic dialogue corpora to the behavior distribution of a reference corpus. GroupPersona turns population statistics into generation controls: it separates each dialogue's core behavioral signature from predictable side effects, and uses the resulting behavioral groups to condition user agents on the interaction patterns that define the reference population. We evaluate GroupPersona on four corpora crossing two dialogue sources, assistant-style and Reddit-derived, with two construction variants: structure-preserving and variation-enhanced. GroupPersona lowers Jensen-Shannon divergence between synthetic and reference distributions over 12 behavior attributes from 0.234 to 0.177 relative to the strongest average baseline, a 24.4% reduction, and is best or tied-best on all four corpora while preserving structural alignment. It also achieves the closest calibration to reference-conversation quality scores, reducing mean absolute deviation from the reference-conversation profile to 0.63 versus 0.91 for the next-best baseline.

</details>


### [86] [ROSUM-MCTS: Monte Carlo Tree Search-Inspired HDL Code Summarization with Structural Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07925)
*Prashanth Vijayaraghavan,Charles Mackin,Luyao Shi,Apoorva Nitsure,Ashutosh Jadhav,David Beymer,Tyler Baldwin,Ehsan Degan,Vandana Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ROSUN-MCTS：一种基于蒙特卡洛树搜索的LLM引导方法，通过结构化探索和强化学习优化来改进硬件描述语言（VHDL/Verilog）的代码摘要生成，在功能和内容质量上均超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在代码摘要任务中表现出潜力，但其在硬件描述语言（如VHDL和Verilog）上的有效性尚未得到充分探索，需要专门的方法来解决HDL代码摘要的独特挑战。

Method: 提出ROSUN-MCTS方法，受蒙特卡洛树搜索启发，通过层次化候选扩展机制整合局部和全局上下文，并使用复合奖励函数（平衡功能正确性、局部内容充分性和流畅性）进行强化驱动的优化。

Result: 在VHDL-eval和Verilog-eval数据集上，ROSUN-MCTS一致优于基线方法，对变量重命名等表面修改具有鲁棒性。消融研究证实了局部和全局扩展策略的必要性，以及平衡功能正确性和内容充分性的重要性。

Conclusion: ROSUN-MCTS是一个有效且鲁棒的HDL代码摘要框架，为强化学习增强的代码摘要研究铺平了道路，展现了在硬件描述语言摘要任务中的优越性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in code summarization, yet their effectiveness for Hardware Description Languages (HDLs) like VHDL and Verilog remains underexplored. We propose ROSUM-MCTS, an LLM-guided approach inspired by Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) that refines summaries through structured exploration and reinforcement-driven optimization. Our method integrates both local and global context via a hierarchical candidate expansion mechanism and optimizes summaries using a composite reward function balancing functional correctness (FC), local content adequacy (LCA), and fluency. We evaluate ROSUM-MCTS on the VHDL-eval and Verilog-eval datasets, demonstrating its consistent outperformance over baseline methods by leveraging structured bottom-up refinement and reinforcement-based optimization. Ablation studies confirm the necessity of both local and global expansion strategies, as well as the importance of balancing FC and LCA for optimal performance. Furthermore, ROSUM-MCTS proves robust against superficial modifications, such as variable renaming, maintaining summary quality where baselines degrade. These results establish ROSUM-MCTS as an effective and robust HDL summarization framework, paving the way for further research into reinforcement-enhanced code summarization.

</details>


### [87] [Illusions of the Gold Standard: A Large-scale Analysis of Human Evaluation Protocols for Long-form Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07936)
*Katelyn Xiaoying Mei,Yi-Li Hsu,Minjoon Choi,Zongwan Cao,Chenjun Xu,Bingbing Wen,Su Lin Blodgett,Lucy Lu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对2023-2025年*CL会议中284篇论文的人工评估协议进行大规模分析，发现评估报告存在广泛不透明问题，并提出了改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工评估在生成文本质量评估中至关重要，但目前实践中评估协议的透明度和文档记录不足，影响评估的可靠性和可复现性。

Method: 对2023-2025年*CL会议中长文本生成任务的人工评估协议进行大规模分析，包括对284篇论文进行人工审阅，以及对1800+篇论文进行LLM辅助分析，定义了20个与可复现性相关的可报告标准。

Result: 发现人工评估研究设计中存在广泛的不透明报告问题，导致评估内容、方法、评估者身份和评估结果解释等方面存在模糊性。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现提出了可操作的建议，以支持未来研究中更透明和可复现的报告，并公开了分析代码和标注数据集。

Abstract: Human evaluation plays a critical role in assessing the quality of generated text. However, the reliability and reproducibility of these evaluations depend on transparent and well-documented protocols -- details that are frequently missing in current practice. In this work, we conduct a large-scale analysis of human evaluation protocols for evaluating long-form generation tasks in *CL conference publications from 2023--2025, including a full manual review of 284 papers and LLM-assisted analysis for another 1.8k+ papers. We define a set of 20 reportable criteria related to reproducibility of human evaluation studies, and apply these criteria to systematically examine reporting norms and practices within the community. We find widespread under-reporting of important aspects of human evaluation study design, leading to ambiguity about what was measured and how, who contributed judgments, and how judgments should be interpreted. Based on these findings, we outline actionable recommendations to support more transparent and reproducible reporting in future research. Our analysis code and annotated dataset can be found at: https://github.com/larchlab/Illusions-of-the-Gold-Standard

</details>


### [88] [From `May' to `Is': Certainty Distortion in Language Model Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07951)
*Catarina G Belem,Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Mark Steyvers,Sameer Singh,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型在保留语义内容时存在确定性失真现象，即表达确定性发生有意义的改变，这种失真在科学和医学领域任务中高达75%的输出，且系统性地偏向增加确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人们越来越多地依赖语言模型(LMs)来讨论、改写和总结科学文章、新闻和医学报告等信息，在这些表达确定性很重要的领域中，尚不清楚语言模型是否能够忠实地保留原始确定性水平。

Method: 提出了一个与人群层面确定性判断一致的语言模型评估指标，使用该指标在不同的模型规模和模型家族中，对科学和医学交流任务中的确定性失真进行系统表征。

Result: 确定性失真影响高达75%的语言模型输出，在改写任务中表现出系统不对称性：大多数语言模型增加表达确定性的可能性比减少的可能性高1.5-2倍。在医学领域中，经过5次迭代改写后，确定性增加的比例从20%上升到40%。基于提示的干预措施可以减少但无法完全消除确定性失真。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了语言模型普遍存在夸大表达确定性的偏差，这对依赖语言模型处理高风险领域的用户具有直接的影响和警示意义。

Abstract: Humans increasingly turn to Language Models (LMs) in ways that shape beliefs and drive decisions, including discussing, rewriting, and summarizing information from scientific articles, news, and medical reports. However, in these domains, where how confidently a claim is expressed matters, little is known about whether LMs faithfully preserve it. In this work, we investigate certainty distortion in LMs, defined as meaningful changes in expressed certainty when semantic content is preserved. We propose an LM-based evaluation metric that is consistent with population-level judgments of certainty. Using this metric, we characterize certainty distortion across different sizes and families of models in the context of scientific and medical communication tasks. Our results show that certainty distortion affects up to 75\% of LM outputs and is systematically asymmetric in rewriting tasks with most LMs being 1.5-2$\times$ more likely to increase the expressed certainty than to decrease it. These effects can compound over repeated paraphrasing: in the medical domain, claude-haiku-4-5 increases certainty of 20\% examples after a single iteration, increasing to 40\% after five iterations. Prompt-based interventions reduce overall certainty distortion but do not eliminate it. Together, these findings reveal a general bias toward inflating expressed certainty, with direct implications for users who rely on LMs in high-stakes domains.

</details>


### [89] [What Does Debiasing Really Remove? A Geometric Study of PCA-Based Gender Debiasing in Word Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07964)
*Alexey Kresin,Tchifou M. Dieffi,Tomer Caspi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PCA去偏方法主要消除第一主成分中的直接性别偏见，但无法有效去除分布式的关联偏见，同时会导致嵌入几何结构退化，存在偏见减少与语义保持之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前广泛使用的基于PCA的性别去偏方法虽然被普遍应用，但其实际去偏效果和破坏性尚不明确。研究者希望系统分析PCA去偏方法到底从嵌入空间中移除了什么，验证偏见是否真的集中在低维子空间这一假设。

Method: 采用系统的几何分析方法，通过多组嵌入实验，研究PCA去偏过程中的几何变化。具体包括：分析直接性别偏见在主成分中的分布，测量关联偏见（使用WEAT测试）与主方向的对齐程度，评估移除不同数量主成分对嵌入几何结构的破坏效果。

Result: 直接性别偏见主要集中在第一主成分，支持低秩偏见假设；但关联偏见并不与这些主方向对齐，而是分布在多个嵌入维度；移除更多主成分会导致嵌入几何结构持续退化，影响语义结构和向量关系；PCA去偏存在权衡：有效减少直接偏见但无法消除分布式关联偏见，同时引入几何扭曲；不存在普遍最优的去偏水平，平衡点取决于所选指标和嵌入。

Conclusion: 词嵌入中的偏见并非纯粹的低秩性质，简单的子空间移除方法可能不足以实现全面的去偏。PCA去偏方法虽然在减少直接偏见方面有效，但无法处理分布式关联偏见，并会造成语义信息的损失，需要更全面的去偏策略。

Abstract: Debiasing methods based on principal component analysis (PCA) are broadly used to reduce gender bias in word embeddings used in LLMs, yet it remains unclear what aspects of bias they actually remove and how destructive this process is. These methods are based on the understanding that bias resides in a low-dimensional subspace, with the assumption that most of it can be captured by a few principal components. In this work, we conduct a systematic geometric analysis of PCA-based gender debiasing and investigate what is actually removed from the embedding space. Our experiments across multiple embeddings show that direct gender bias is primarily concentrated in the first principal component, supporting the low-rank bias hypothesis. However, associative bias measured by WEAT does not align with these principal directions and is instead spread across multiple embedding dimensions. Furthermore, as expected, we demonstrate that removing an increasing number of principal components leads to a consistent degradation of the embedding geometry, affecting semantic structure and vector relationships. These results reveal that PCA-based debiasing operates as a trade-off: while it effectively reduces certain forms of direct bias, it fails to eliminate distributed associations and introduces geometric distortion. Moreover, there is no universal optimal level of debiasing, as the balance between bias reduction and semantic preservation depends on the chosen metric and embedding. Overall, our findings suggest that bias in word embeddings is not purely low-rank and that simple subspace removal methods may be insufficient for comprehensive debiasing.

</details>


### [90] [Neutrality Bites: Gender Representation in AI-Generated Animal Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07969)
*Imani Finkley,Yuanxi Li,Melanie Walsh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现在AI生成的故事中，当需要为未明确性别的拟人化动物角色分配性别时，大型语言模型展现出明显的男性偏向，而追求"中性"策略实际上可能导致边缘化身份的抹除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然AI生成故事中的性别偏见已得到广泛关注，但现有干预措施是否真正产生更公平结果尚不明确。研究旨在探究大型语言模型在高度模糊、容易再现人类刻板印象的叙事情境（拟人化动物故事）中如何处理性别分配问题。

Method: 研究使用6个领先的大型语言模型，让它们完成关于7个未明确性别的拟人化动物角色的英语故事。实验设置了4种不同的叙事背景和一系列模型温度参数，共生成23,800个故事进行分析。

Result: 研究发现：1) 模型经常避免为动物角色分配性别（平均19%）；2) 经常使用性别中性语言如"it"或"its"（平均38.2%）；3) 当分配性别时存在显著的男性偏见，女性角色仅出现在2.2%的故事中，而男性角色出现在40.6%的故事中。

Conclusion: 研究提出"中立性具有两面性"的论点：模型为了应对社会偏见而优先考虑中立性，实际上可能加剧边缘化视角和身份的抹除。需要超越中立性策略，探索更平等分配社会可能性给想象主体的替代方法。

Abstract: Gender bias in AI-generated stories is a well-documented problem. While much attention has been paid to reducing or mitigating this bias, it is not always clear whether interventions produce genuinely fairer results. To investigate this issue, we examine how large language models (LLMs) handle gender assignment in a narrative context that is popular, highly ambiguous, and also known to closely reproduce human stereotypes: stories about talking animals. We prompt six leading LLMs to complete an English-language story about seven different anthropomorphic animal characters whose gender is unstated. We additionally iterate with four different narrative settings and a range of model temperatures. Across the 23.8K stories, we find that models frequently avoid gendering the animal character in the story (19% on average) or use gender-neutral language like "it" or "its" (38.2% on average). However, when gender is assigned, there is a significant masculine bias. Feminine animal characters are virtually absent, present in just 2.2% of stories vs. 40.6% that feature masculine characters. Our findings point to a broader argument: neutrality bites. In other words, models that prioritize neutrality to address social bias may actually contribute to the erasure of marginalized perspectives and identities. We suggest that alternative strategies beyond neutrality need to be pursued, such as ones that more equally distribute social possibilities across imagined subjects.

</details>


### [91] [Defending Against Malicious Finetuning by Scaling Train-time Adversarial Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07970)
*Haoming Wen,Shi Chen,Qingyu Shi,Siyuan Liu,Minrui Luo,Jingzhao Zhang,Tianxing He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Patcher：一种防御恶意全参数微调攻击的对抗训练方法，通过双级优化和并行算法提升LLM安全性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开源权重大语言模型容易受到恶意微调攻击，攻击者仅需少量有监督微调就能破坏模型的安全对齐。现有对齐阶段防御方法主要针对参数高效微调攻击设计，无法防御更强的全参数微调攻击。

Method: Patcher方法受对抗训练和双级优化启发，通过增加对抗循环中的优化步数来增强模拟攻击，迫使防御者找到对强攻击不敏感的模型参数。同时提出高效的并行算法来减少训练时间。

Result: 大量实验表明，与普通有监督微调对齐相比，Patcher显著提升了模型的鲁棒性，并能迁移到不同的攻击场景和模型规模中。代码已开源。

Conclusion: Patcher方法有效防御了针对LLM的恶意全参数微调攻击，通过对抗训练和高效并行实现提升了模型的安全性对齐能力。

Abstract: Current open-weight large language models (LLMs) are prone to malicious finetuning attacks, which could compromise the safety alignment of LLMs with only a few steps of supervised finetuning (SFT) on poisoned datasets. Existing alignment-stage defenses are primarily designed to defend against attacks that use parameter-efficient finetuning methods. However, they fail to defend against stronger attacks that use full-parameter finetuning. In this paper, we propose Patcher, a method inspired by adversarial training and bi-level optimization, to combat such attacks. Patcher strengthens the simulated attack by scaling up the optimization steps in the adversarial loop, thus forcing the defender to find model parameters that are insensitive to stronger attacks. Furthermore, we propose an efficient parallel algorithm to implement Patcher, decreasing the wall-clock time of training while preserving Patcher's performance. Extensive experiments show that Patcher substantially improves the model's robustness compared to vanilla SFT alignment, and transfers to diverse attack scenarios and model sizes. Code is available at https://github.com/haomingwen/patcher.

</details>


### [92] [MechLens: Late Crystallization of Factual Knowledge Explains Intervention Effectiveness in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07978)
*Xueping Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究发现，LLM中的事实知识不是逐层逐渐形成的，而是在最后几层"突然结晶"（Late Crystallization）。这一现象在不同模型架构和规模中普遍存在，为减轻幻觉提供了新的干预原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解LLM中事实知识的存储位置对于减轻幻觉至关重要。目前不清楚知识是在模型中逐渐形成还是在特定位置突然出现。

Method: 系统量化分析了五个模型家族（Pythia、Gemma、Qwen2.5、Llama-3.1、Mistral；0.5-14B）在不同深度层的事实知识出现模式。使用tuned lens排除探测伪影，通过情感分类控制实验验证现象特异性，并进行层归一化消融实验。

Result: 发现26.8%-93.4%的正确答案从未进入任何中间层的前10预测；知识在深度>80%处突然出现；结晶引导的干预原则（CAA）在中等结晶度模型上优于DoLa；层归一化缩放（x1.2）可使MC1性能提升11.8%；揭示了计算性知识（22.1/28层）比记忆性事实（28.0/28层）结晶更早的"计算-记忆谱系"。

Conclusion: 事实知识在LLM中不是逐渐形成而是"晚期结晶"；这一现象具有普遍性和特异性；结晶位置提供了有效的干预点（如层归一化缩放）；不同类型的知识（计算性与记忆性）结晶时间不同；研究为理解和干预LLM的事实召回提供了新视角和工具。

Abstract: Understanding where LLMs store factual knowledge is critical for hallucination mitigation. We systematically quantify Late Crystallization: factual knowledge does not gradually emerge across layers but "crystallizes" abruptly at the final layers. Across five model families (Pythia, Gemma, Qwen2.5, Llama-3.1, Mistral; 0.5--14B), 26.8%--93.4% of correct answers never enter top-10 predictions at any intermediate layer, with late emergence (>80% depth) consistent across architectures. Cross-scale (Qwen2.5-14B) and cross-benchmark (MMLU: 98.2%) results confirm generality; tuned lens rules out probe artifacts. A sentiment-classification control (0.5% for Qwen vs. 85.9% factual; 2.0% for Mistral vs. 26.8%) confirms the phenomenon is specific to factual recall.
  Late Crystallization yields a crystallization-guided intervention principle: CAA outperforms DoLa on moderate-crystallization models (Llama, Mistral; p<0.001), with a directionally consistent reversal on high-crystallization Qwen (+25.4% vs. +15.5% MC1, p=0.069). LayerNorm ablation shows crystallization is intrinsic to the residual stream; LN scaling (x1.2) yields +11.8% MC1 with zero inference overhead. We further reveal a Computability-Memorization Spectrum: computable knowledge crystallizes earlier (layer 22.1/28) than memorized facts (28.0/28). We release MechLens supporting five model families.

</details>


### [93] [Customer-Agent: Overcoming Context Limitations in Ultra-Long Shopping Trajectories via Tool-Augmented Agents and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07995)
*Hongye Liu,Rongmei Lin,Anurag Kashyap,Hejie Cui,Ricardo Henao,Besnik Fetahu,Bing Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出ShopTrajQA长上下文评估基准和顾客代理框架，通过RLVR代理训练实现超长购物轨迹管理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界购物轨迹跨越多年，形成极长序列，现有LLM难以处理；现有基准局限于短轨迹，且真实电商数据因隐私限制难以获取

Method: 1) 构建ShopTrajQA基准数据集（含32k和64k token变体）；2) 提出顾客代理框架，采用强化学习与可验证奖励训练代理，通过代码解释器交互自主检索和解析外部存储的轨迹文件

Result: 基准测试揭示了LLM在长购物轨迹推理上的性能差距；所提框架在ShopTrajQA上表现优异，并能泛化至其他复杂推理任务

Conclusion: 该研究为长轨迹数据管理提供了有效解决方案，通过外部存储和代码交互突破LLM固定上下文窗口限制，支持个性化购物体验

Abstract: Understanding customer shopping trajectories is essential for enabling personalized shopping experiences. However, shopping records (i.e., customer's search, clicks, purchases, etc.) often span long time horizons over multiple years, resulting in extremely long trajectories that pose significant challenges for existing large language models (LLMs). Despite the importance of this problem, existing benchmarks are limited to short customer trajectories, while real-world trajectories from large e-commerce platforms are rarely accessible due to data privacy constraints. To address this gap, we introduce ShopTrajQA, a long-context evaluation benchmark constructed from real-world product information and simulated shopping trajectories. The dataset includes variants of up to 32k and 64k tokens, enabling systematic evaluation of model robustness under varying context lengths. Through comprehensive benchmarking of frontier LLMs, we identify critical performance gaps in reasoning over long shopping trajectory data. To address these challenges, we propose a Customer Agent Framework for ultra-long context management. Leveraging a Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) agentic training paradigm, our approach stores trajectories as external local files and trains the agent to autonomously retrieve and parse them through code-interpreter interactions (e.g., SQL queries), effectively bypassing the fixed in-context window constraints of LLMs. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework achieves strong performance for ShopTrajQA and shows generalization to other complex reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [94] [MC-PDD: Masked Corpus-Level Pretraining Data Detection for Black-Box Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07996)
*Kaixin Lan,Mu You,Tao Fang,Binkai Ou,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MC-PDD是一种针对封闭源大型语言模型的预训练数据检测方法，通过掩码文本预测和统计显著性分析来确定特定数据集是否被用于模型预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练数据的不透明性给大语言模型的分析带来伦理、法律和公平性担忧。现有方法需要访问模型概率分布，不适用于仅提供输入输出接口的封闭源LLMs，因此需要开发适合黑盒环境的检测方法。

Method: 受掩码语言建模启发，MC-PDD方法掩码文本中的高特异性标记，提示LLM预测缺失内容，然后比较候选语料库和参考非成员语料库之间的预测命中率差异，通过统计显著性分析判断候选文本是否可能包含在模型预训练数据中。

Result: 实验结果表明，在三个数据集上，对开源和封闭源LLMs，预训练数据与未见过数据之间存在清晰一致的预测命中率差异。在更严格的黑盒设置下，MC-PDD性能与现有检测方法相当。

Conclusion: MC-PDD实现了仅通过标准API访问即可进行模型审计和数据版权验证的实际应用，为封闭源LLMs的预训练数据检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Pretraining is fundamental to the development of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the opacity of pretraining data complicates model analysis and raises ethical, legal, and fairness concerns. Detecting whether specific datasets were used during pretraining is, therefore, critical. Existing state-of-the-art methods typically rely on access to model probability distributions, making them unsuitable for closed-source LLMs that provide only input-output interfaces. To address this limitation, we introduce Masked Corpus-level Pretraining Data Detection (MC-PDD), a novel method inspired by the masked language modeling paradigm. MC-PDD masks highly specific tokens in each text and prompts the LLM to predict the missing content. It then assesses whether the difference in prediction hit rates between a candidate corpus and a reference non-member corpus is statistically significant. Based on this comparison, MC-PDD determines whether the candidate texts were likely included in the model's pretraining data. Experimental results demonstrate clear and consistent differences in prediction hit rates between pretrained and unseen data across three datasets, for both open-source and closed-source LLMs. Despite operating under a stricter black-box setting, MC-PDD achieves performance comparable to existing detection methods. Our approach enables practical applications such as model auditing and data copyright verification using only standard API access. Upon acceptance, we will publicly release the code and datasets.

</details>


### [95] [Summarization is Not Dead Yet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08000)
*Dongqi Liu,Chenxi Whitehouse,Zheng Zhao,Zhuchen Cao,Jian Li,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过多维评估重新审视了LLM在文本摘要任务上的表现，发现人类参考摘要仍在信息丰富度和忠实度方面保持优势，而LLM输出主要在表面连贯性和流畅度上受到青睐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型在文本摘要任务上的进展，有观点认为模型生成的摘要已匹敌甚至超越人类参考摘要，这引发了"摘要生成是否仍为开放研究问题"的疑问。作者旨在通过综合评估重新审视这一观点。

Method: 采用多维度评估方法：覆盖五个不同数据集和五种最先进的LLM；结合控制性人类评估、偏差缓解的LLM-as-Judge协议、基于外部知识的事实性验证以及语料级语言分析。

Result: 研究发现人类参考摘要继续在信息丰富度和忠实度方面表现出优势，而LLM输出主要在表面连贯性和流畅度上更受青睐。事实性验证显示人类参考更可靠，特别是在涉及推理或综合的陈述中；语言分析揭示了不同模型间的风格同质化模式。

Conclusion: 当前LLMs提高了摘要质量的基线，但其性能上限仍低于人类能力。虽然LLMs在表面质量上有进步，但在关键的质量维度上仍未达到人类水平，因此文本摘要仍是一个活跃的研究领域。

Abstract: The progress of large language models (LLMs) has fueled claims that model-generated summaries rival or even surpass human-written references, raising questions about whether summarization remains an open research problem. We re-examine this narrative through a multi-track evaluation covering five diverse datasets and five state-of-the-art LLMs, combining controlled human assessment, bias-mitigated LLM-as-Judge protocols, factuality verification against external knowledge, and corpus-level linguistic analysis. Our findings reveal a more nuanced landscape in which human reference summaries continue to demonstrate advantages in informativeness and faithfulness, whereas LLM outputs are preferred mainly for surface-level coherence and fluency. Factuality verification indicates that human references remain more reliable, particularly for claims involving reasoning or synthesis, and linguistic analysis uncovers a pattern of stylistic homogeneity across different models. These observations suggest that current LLMs have raised the floor of summarization quality, but the ceiling of their performance remains below human capabilities.

</details>


### [96] [Rewrite to Translate, Translate to Reward: Reinforcement Learning for Source Rewriting in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08011)
*Boxuan Lyu,Haiyue Song,Zhi Qu,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RLSR是一种基于强化学习的源文本重写框架，无需为每个MT模型手动调优提示词，直接使用下游翻译质量的提升作为奖励来优化重写模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管直接提示现成的大型语言模型（LLMs）生成保持语义的源文本重写可以有效提高机器翻译（MT）质量，但这需要为不同的MT模型手动调优提示词，过程繁琐且效率低下

Method: RLSR基于强化学习框架训练源文本重写模型：使用每个重写后源文本带来的下游翻译质量改进作为奖励信号，直接优化重写模型；无需为每个MT模型定制提示词

Result: 在6个MT模型和16个语言对上进行的广泛实验表明：通过RLSR训练的4B重写模型显著优于不进行重写的基线和现有同等规模的基于提示的重写基线，同时在与基于235B LLM的提示基线比较中达到竞争性性能

Conclusion: RLSR框架提供了一种无需手动调优提示词的源文本重写方法，有效提高了机器翻译质量，在小规模模型上实现了与大型模型相似的性能

Abstract: Although directly prompting off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate meaning-preserving source rewrites can effectively enhance Machine Translation (MT) quality, doing so requires manually tuning prompts for different MT models. In this work, we propose RLSR (Reinforcement Learning for Source Rewriting), a novel RL-based framework for training a source rewriting model without tuning prompts for each MT model. RLSR optimizes the rewriting model by directly using the improvement in downstream translation quality yielded by each rewritten source as the reward. Extensive experiments across six MT models and 16 language pairs demonstrate that our 4B rewriting models trained via RLSR significantly outperform the no-rewriting baseline and existing same-scale prompt-based rewriting baselines, while achieving competitive performance against prompt-based baselines based on the 235B LLM.

</details>


### [97] [Arabic Sentence Segmentation Across Genres and Punctuation Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08025)
*Mohammed Elkholy,Khalid N. Elmadani,Nizar Habash,Bashar Alhafni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AraSEG：针对阿拉伯语句子分割挑战的新语料库，包含八种文本体裁，评估发现轻量级编码器模型在最具挑战性的场景下优于大型语言模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语句子分割具有挑战性，因为标点符号模糊且不一致，许多文本缺乏可靠的句子边界标记。现有方法严重依赖标点符号线索，且通常在格式良好的文本上评估，限制了在真实阿拉伯语环境中的鲁棒性。

Method: 引入AraSEG语料库，涵盖八种文本体裁和广泛的标点符号及文档结构条件。使用该语料库评估LLMs、轻量级编码器模型和基于依存解析器的模型，研究训练数据规模和体裁多样性对性能的影响。

Result: 轻量级编码器模型甚至基于依存解析器的模型在最具挑战性的设置中优于LLMs。性能最终会饱和，跨体裁泛化仍然具有挑战性。准确的句子分割显著改善了下游依存解析性能。

Conclusion: 需要针对现实世界阿拉伯语文本的鲁棒句子分割方法，现有方法在标点符号模糊的挑战性场景中表现不佳。AraSEG语料库为解决这一挑战提供了重要资源，展示了轻量级模型在此任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Sentence segmentation in Arabic is challenging due to ambiguous and inconsistent punctuation, with many texts lacking reliable sentence boundary markers. Existing approaches rely heavily on punctuation cues and are typically evaluated on well-formed text, limiting their robustness in realistic Arabic settings. To address this, we introduce AraSEG, a genre-diverse sentence segmentation corpus spanning eight genres and a wide range of punctuation and document structure conditions. Using AraSEG, we evaluate LLMs, lightweight encoder models, and dependency parser-based models under increasingly challenging segmentation settings. Our experiments show that lightweight encoders, and even dependency parser-based models, outperform LLMs in the most challenging settings. We further investigate the effects of training data size and genre diversity, finding that performance eventually saturates and cross-genre generalization remains challenging. We also demonstrate that accurate sentence segmentation substantially improves downstream dependency parsing. We make our code, data, and models publicly available.

</details>


### [98] [Diffusion Language Model Parallel Decoding via Product-of-Experts Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08048)
*Juntong Shi,Brian L. Trippe,Jure Leskovec,Stefano Ermon,Minkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PoE-Bridge是一种新颖的解码框架，通过引入中间分布作为扩散语言模型和自回归模型之间的桥梁，显著提高了生成速度和质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型虽然通过并行解码具有速度优势，但由于缺乏token依赖性，生成质量不如自回归模型。现有的重要性采样方法由于分布差异大，需要大量粒子计算，成本高昂

Method: 提出PoE-Bridge解码框架：1）构建DLM提议分布和AR目标分布的专家乘积作为中间分布；2）使用DLM并行起草多个延续；3）应用拒绝采样验证起草的token并将候选向PoE对齐；4）使用重要性采样进一步将PoE对齐的候选校正到AR目标；5）提出混合温度采样增强多样性，弹性拒绝窗口减少浪费验证

Result: PoE-Bridge在标准DLM解码方法上实现了5倍加速，恢复了至少95%的目标AR模型性能，在数学推理和编码任务上高效缩小了大部分质量差距

Conclusion: PoE-Bridge通过引入专家乘积作为中间分布，有效桥接了DLM和AR模型之间的分布差距，在保持并行解码速度优势的同时显著提升了生成质量

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer substantial speed advantages through parallel decoding, but the lack of token dependencies limits generation quality compared to autoregressive (AR) models. Recent progress attempts to bridge the gap via importance sampling, with DLM being the proposal and AR being the target. However, due to the huge gap between their distributions, the sampling requires a large number of particles and is thus expensive to compute. In this paper, we introduce PoE-Bridge, a novel decoding framework that drastically improves generation speed and accuracy by introducing an intermediate distribution to bridge the gap. The distribution is constructed as a Product-of-Experts (PoE) of the DLM proposal and the AR target. With the intermediate distribution, we first use the DLM to draft multiple continuations in parallel, then apply rejection sampling to verify the drafted tokens and move the resulting candidates toward the PoE. We then use importance sampling to further correct the PoE-aligned candidates toward the AR target. We further propose several improved techniques, including mixed-temperature sampling for enhanced diversity and elastic rejection windows for reducing wasted verification. Empirically, PoE-Bridge achieves significantly improved accuracy with $5\times$ speedup over the standard DLM decoding approach, and recovers at least 95% of the target AR model's performance, efficiently advancing most of the quality gap on challenging mathematical reasoning and coding tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/juntongshi48/poe-bridge.

</details>


### [99] [What's the Point? Spatial Grammar & Index Resolution for Sign Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08056)
*Oline Ranum,Simon Hadfield,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种针对手语空间索引（指向手势）的评估和建模框架，通过检测索引和话语实体链接，改善传统基于词汇序列或文本监督的手语模型对非词汇结构的建模不足问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手语模型主要基于词汇序列或文本监督进行训练，忽视了非词汇和构词结构。空间索引（指向手势将话语实体分配给空间位置以便后续指代）是一个相对可处理的实例，但词汇中心的目标在很大程度上无法捕捉这种结构。

Method: 提出了一个训练和评估索引专家的框架：将空间指代解析分解为索引检测和话语实体链接；生成的话语表示可用于自动标注和非词汇结构建模；作为辅助索引专家，在推理时增强冻结的手语识别模型。

Result: 研究显示，尽管索引占手语内容的10-15%，但在手语识别中恢复效果很差；建立了索引感知手语建模的基线；提出的框架能够更好地建模非词汇结构，并通过辅助专家改进冻结模型性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为手语空间索引的建模提供了系统框架，通过专门处理指向手势的表示和链接，显著改善了手语识别系统对非词汇结构的捕捉能力，为更全面的手语建模奠定了基础。

Abstract: Sign language models are predominantly trained with gloss-sequence or text supervision, thereby under-modeling non-lexical and productive constructions. One comparatively tractable instance is spatial indexing: pointing gestures that assign discourse entities to spatial loci for subsequent co-reference, which lexicon-centric objectives largely fail to capture. We present a targeted evaluation of indexing in Sign Language Recognition, showing that despite comprising 10-15% of signing content, indexing is poorly recovered. We introduce a framework for training and evaluating indexing experts, establishing a baseline for index-aware sign language modeling. Our approach decomposes spatial reference resolution into index detection and discourse entity linking. The resulting mention representations enable automatic annotation and non-lexical structure modeling, and serve as an auxiliary indexing expert that augments a frozen SLR model at inference time.

</details>


### [100] [SurgiQ: A Large-Scale Multi-Domain Benchmark for Evaluating Surgical Understanding in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08071)
*Ayah Al-Naji,Edoardo Fazzari,Saif Alkindi,Hamdan Alhadhrami,Preslav Nakov,Cesare Stefanini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SurgiQ是一个包含13,055道四选项多选题的手术领域基准测试，涵盖六个外科领域和四种题型，用于评估LLM在手术推理方面的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可靠评估大型语言模型在外科领域能力的方法不足。广泛的医学基准测试主要测试临床知识，而外科手术需要程序性推理、管理权衡、否定处理以及在多种合理手术决策中进行选择的能力。

Method: 从外科教科书、开放获取论文和考试材料中，通过多阶段生成、验证和专家审核流程构建SurgiQ基准。使用统一的log-likelihood协议评估35个开源LLM。

Result: 评估结果显示：较小模型接近25%的随机基线，最佳模型达到68.1%准确率；通用模型（特别是Qwen2.5）优于大多数生物医学模型；即使强模型也会在临床合理的干扰项上犯自信的错误。

Conclusion: 当前医学专门化尚未提供足够广泛的外科覆盖，需要更可靠和更广泛的外科LLM评估方法，以解决模型在临床合理干扰项上自信犯错的问题。

Abstract: Reliable evaluation of large language models in surgery remains underdeveloped. Broad medical benchmarks test clinical knowledge, while surgery requires procedural reasoning, management trade-offs, negation handling, and selection among plausible operative decisions. We present SurgiQ, a text-only, source-grounded benchmark of 13,055 four-option multiple-choice questions spanning six surgical domains and four question formats: case-based, reasoning, best-option, and negative. SurgiQ is constructed from surgical textbooks, open-access papers, and examination material using a multi-stage generation, verification, and expert-audit pipeline. We evaluate 35 open-weight LLMs under a unified log-likelihood protocol. Our results show substantial remaining headroom: smaller models often remain near the 25\% random baseline, while the best model reaches 68.1\% accuracy. General-purpose models, especially Qwen2.5, outperform most biomedical models, suggesting that current medical specialization does not yet provide sufficiently broad surgical coverage. Calibration and error analysis further show that even strong models make confident mistakes on clinically plausible distractors, motivating more reliable and broader surgical LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [101] ["I understand your perspective": LLM Persuasion and Sycophancy through the Lens of Communicative Action Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08076)
*Esra Dönmez,Agnieszka Falenska*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型在说服性沟通中的表现，发现LLMs不仅能够表达言外之意意图，甚至比人类更擅长这种微妙的沟通策略，这增加了它们的人类化感知和说服力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然大型语言模型能够生成高质量论点，但它们在细微且具说服力的沟通行为方面的能力尚未得到充分探索。研究旨在通过尤尔根·哈贝马斯的交往行为理论框架，考察LLMs是否能够以类似人类的方式表达言外之意意图，从而探索它们的说服潜力。

Method: 研究使用ChangeMyView说服性子reddit的对话，模拟意见持有者与LLMs之间的在线讨论。比较人类撰写和LLM生成的反驳论点中言外之意意图的可能性，特别关注那些成功改变原发帖者观点的论点。使用了三种不同的LLMs进行分析。

Result: 研究发现：1）所有三种LLMs都能有效传达言外之意意图，甚至比人类更擅长；2）LLMs会生成迎合性回应，紧密符合意见持有者的意图，这种策略与观点改变密切相关；3）众包工作者发现LLM生成的反驳论点更易接受且更受青睐。

Conclusion: LLMs的说服力不仅限于生成高质量论点。相反，通过人类偏好训练的LLMs能够有效模拟人类沟通模式，特别是细微的沟通行为，这可能会增加个体对其影响的易感性，表明LLMs在说服性沟通中具有显著的潜力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate high-quality arguments, yet their ability to engage in nuanced and persuasive communicative actions remains largely unexplored. This work explores the persuasive potential of LLMs through the framework of Jürgen Habermas' Theory of Communicative Action. It examines whether LLMs express illocutionary intent (i.e., pragmatic functions of language such as conveying knowledge, building trust, or signaling similarity) in ways that are comparable to human communication. We simulate online discussions between opinion holders and LLMs using conversations from the persuasive subreddit ChangeMyView. We then compare the likelihood of illocutionary intents in human-written and LLM-generated counter-arguments, specifically those that successfully changed the original poster's view. We find that all three LLMs effectively convey illocutionary intent -- often more so than humans -- potentially increasing their anthropomorphism. Further, LLMs craft sycophantic responses that closely align with the opinion holder's intent, a strategy strongly associated with opinion change. Finally, crowd-sourced workers find LLM-generated counter-arguments more agreeable and consistently prefer them over human-written ones. These findings suggest that LLMs' persuasive power extends beyond merely generating high-quality arguments. On the contrary, training LLMs with human preferences effectively tunes them to mirror human communication patterns, particularly nuanced communicative actions, potentially increasing individuals' susceptibility to their influence.

</details>


### [102] [Support Vector Rubrics: Closing the Gap Between Self-Generated and Human Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08077)
*Mengyuan Sun,Yu Li,Zhuohao Yu,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SVR（支持向量规则）框架通过将规则构建重新构造为偏好数据上的最大边界学习，以缩小自生成规则与人类标注标准之间的判别差距。该方法从偏好对中挖掘对比特征到规则库，学习提示条件选择器和全局规则权重，并通过支持对选择和硬负例对抗探测迭代优化规则库。在推理时，仅给定提示即可检索顶部规则并评分响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的评估是评估大语言模型输出的有前景范式，但自生成规则在困难实例上滞后于人类标注标准。这种判别差距反映了目标不匹配：自生成规则描述好的响应，而有效的标准必须区分相近候选。需要缩小这一差距。

Method: SVR框架将规则构建重新构造为偏好数据上的最大边界学习。具体方法包括：1）从偏好对中挖掘对比特征构建规则库；2）学习提示条件选择器与全局规则权重；3）通过支持对选择和硬负例对抗探测迭代优化规则库；4）推理时仅需提示即可检索顶部规则评分响应。

Result: 在RubricBench上，SVR将与人参考规则的差距从24.1分缩小至0.3分，优于强基线自规则和评判方法，且学习的规则库无需重新训练即可跨评判器迁移。在RewardBench 1&2和RM-Bench上，与专用奖励模型保持竞争力，展示了更广泛的奖励建模能力。

Conclusion: 定义边界的规则为缩小LLM评估中的判别差距提供了原则性途径。SVR框架通过最大边界学习构建判别性规则，显著改善了规则评估性能，并展示了在奖励建模任务上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Rubric-based evaluation is a promising paradigm for judging large language model (LLM) outputs, yet self-generated rubrics lag human-annotated criteria on hard instances. We argue this discriminative gap reflects an objective mismatch: self-generated rubrics describe good responses, whereas effective criteria must discriminate between close candidates. To close this gap, we introduce SVR (Support Vector Rubrics), a framework that recasts rubric construction as max-margin boundary learning over preference data. SVR mines contrastive features from preference pairs into a rubric bank, learns a prompt-conditioned selector together with global rubric weights, and iteratively refines the bank through support-pair selection and adversarial probing of hard negatives. At inference, given only the prompt, SVR retrieves the top-rubrics from the bank and scores responses. On RubricBench, SVR narrows the gap to human reference rubrics from 24.1 to 0.3 points and outperforms strong self-rubric and judge baselines, and the learned bank transfers across judges without retraining. On RewardBench 1&2, and RM-Bench, it remains competitive with dedicated reward models, demonstrating broader reward modeling capability. Overall, boundary-defining rubrics offer a principled route to closing the discriminative gap in LLM evaluation.

</details>


### [103] [Aligned but Not Partner-Specific: Distinguishing How Multimodal LLM Agents Succeed in Reference Games Without Human-Like Conventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08081)
*Po-Ya Angela Wang,Chinmaya Mishra,Aslı Özyürek,Paula Rubio-Fernández,Esam Ghaleb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在重复指称游戏中虽能实现标签对齐，但无法像人类一样通过互动历史形成简洁的、伙伴特定的指称约定，而是依赖冗长描述来实现协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明多模态LLMs在重复指称游戏中虽然能对齐标签使用，但不会随时间变得更高效。需要区分这种对齐是来自与特定伙伴的互动历史（真实对话基础）还是共享任务词汇，以理解MLLMs是否真正实现了伙伴特定的对话基础。

Method: 提出约束伪对话基线的创新方法，该基线匹配原始指称任务结构但破坏伙伴历史。将多模态智能体对话对与KTH Tangrams语料库中的人类对话对进行比较，从三个分析层面对比：任务能力、描述策略和对齐动态。

Result: 人类通过趋同减少努力，压缩描述并增加与伙伴的标签对齐。而智能体保持固定努力水平，从第一轮开始就产生冗长描述，标签重叠接近天花板，且在真实对话与伪对话之间无统计差异。MLLMs通过冗长描述而非形成紧凑的、历史依赖的指称表达式来成功协调。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型实现了协调但未形成约定，其成功基于冗长描述而非形成人类对话特有的紧凑、历史依赖的指称表达式。这表明MLLMs缺乏真正的对话基础能力，即基于共享互动历史形成伙伴特定的、高效的语言惯例。

Abstract: Repeated reference games test whether interlocutors replace their initially long descriptions with shorter, partner-specific conventions grounded in shared interaction history. Prior work shows that multimodal LLMs fail to become more efficient across rounds, although they align on the labels they use. How can we determine whether this alignment reflects partner-specific grounding rather than a shared task vocabulary? We address this question by comparing capable multimodal agent dyads with human dyads from the KTH Tangrams corpus. Our novel methodological contribution is a constrained pseudo-dyad baseline that matches the original referential task structure, but breaks partner history. This baseline enables us to test whether the observed label alignment depends on interaction with a specific partner. Across three analytic layers (task competence, description strategy, alignment dynamics), we find clear differences. Humans reduce effort through entrainment, compressing descriptions and increasing label alignment with partners. Agents instead maintain fixed effort levels, producing verbose descriptions from round one, with near-ceiling label overlap that is statistically indistinguishable between real and pseudo dyads. MLLMs thus achieve coordination without convention, succeeding by verbose description rather than by forming the compact, history-dependent referring expressions characteristic of human dialogue.

</details>


### [104] [When Languages Disagree: Self-Evolving Multilingual LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08092)
*Xiyan Fu,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SEMJ：一种利用跨语言不一致性进行迭代精化的自进化多语言评估方法，通过多语言变体构建、独立判断收集和不一致输出自我反思，显著提升评估准确性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言LLM-as-a-judge方法将跨语言不一致性视为噪声并通过投票或聚合缓解，但实际上这种不一致性可能提供互补的评估信号。研究发现不同语言中的判断具有互补性，采样多语言判断能获得比单语言评估更高的性能上限。

Method: 提出SEMJ（自进化多语言评估器）方法：1）为每个输入构建多语言变体；2）收集独立的判断和推理依据；3）将不一致的输出反馈给系统进行自我反思和重新评估；4）通过迭代精化利用跨语言不一致性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的实验表明，SEMJ在准确性和跨语言一致性方面持续优于投票和反思基线方法。进一步分析显示，不一致性触发有用的重新评估，从而提高了判断质量。

Conclusion: 跨语言不一致性不仅不是需要消除的噪声，反而可以作为互补的评估信号加以利用。SEMJ通过自进化机制有效利用这种不一致性进行迭代精化，为多语言评估提供了新视角和有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multilingual LLM-as-a-judge is widely used to evaluate model outputs across languages, but suffers from cross-lingual inconsistency (Fu and Liu, 2025). Existing methods typically treat this inconsistency as noise and mitigate it through voting or aggregation. In this work, we instead show that multilingual inconsistency can provide complementary evaluation signals. Our oracle analysis finds that sampling judgments across languages yields a higher performance upper bound than single-language judging, indicating that different languages potentially include complementary judgments. Motivated by this finding, we propose SEMJ, a self-evolving multilingual judge that leverages cross-lingual inconsistency for iterative refinement. SEMJ constructs multilingual variants of each input, collects independent judgments and rationales, and feeds inconsistent outputs back for self-reflection and re-evaluation. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SEMJ consistently outperforms voting and reflection baselines in both accuracy and cross-lingual consistency. Further analysis shows that inconsistency triggers useful re-evaluation, which improves judgment quality.

</details>


### [105] [Cross Paraphrastic Invariance Learning for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08157)
*Shanshan Lin,Dongsheng Hong,Sibo Ju,Chao Chen,Sihong Xie,Xiangwen Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CPIL是一个两阶段孪生网络框架，通过生成释义视图作为正样本和挖掘同文档相反标签对作为硬负样本，在仅有约1%标注数据的情况下实现了卓越的幻觉检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大型语言模型生成的幻觉检测问题，避免成本高昂的LLM作为评估器的流程和现有分类器对大量标注数据的需求。

Method: CPIL采用两阶段孪生网络框架：第一阶段通过对比预训练学习释义不变且具有接地意识的嵌入空间，第二阶段附加轻量级分类器进行二元接地性分类。通过生成文档声明示例的释义视图作为正样本，并挖掘同文档但相反标签的配对作为硬负样本。

Result: 在LLM-AggreFact基准测试（11个任务）上，CPIL在仅使用约1%标注数据的情况下，F1分数超过了强基线方法，展现了其预测优越性和标签效率。

Conclusion: CPIL通过创新的两阶段学习框架，有效利用有限的标注数据，实现了高效且准确的幻觉检测，为解决LLM生成内容的接地性问题提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently generate hallucinations, which are unsupported by a source document. To avoid costly LLM-as-evaluator pipelines and the heavy annotation demands of existing classifiers, we propose CPIL (Cross Paraphrastic Invariance Learning), a two-stage Siamese framework that maximizes the utility of existing labeled data. Concretely, CPIL constructs informative training pairs by: (i) generating paraphrastic views of each document-claim example as positives, and explicitly aligning their representations to enforce invariance to surface form; and (ii) mining same-document, opposite-label pairs as hard negatives to sharpen document-sensitive decision boundaries. Then CPIL conduct a two-stage model training: Stage 1 performs contrastive pretraining to learn a paraphrase-invariant, grounding-aware embedding space; and Stage 2 attaches a lightweight classifier for binary groundedness. On the LLM-AggreFact benchmark (11 tasks), CPIL surpasses strong baselines concerning F1 scores with only ~1% labeled data, showing its prediction superiority and label efficiency.

</details>


### [106] [Constrained Paraphrase Consistency for LLM Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08158)
*Shanshan Lin,Dongsheng Hong,Sibo Ju,Chao Chen,Xi Zhang,Xiangwen Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CCHD：一种基于约束优化的幻觉检测方法，通过引入释义一致性和标签保持约束，在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型会产生事实不一致的陈述（幻觉），需要准确且可扩展的幻觉检测器。现有方法通常通过合成或新标注扩大训练集，成本高且可能引入偏差，同时未能充分利用语义等价释义所隐含的一致性信息。

Method: 提出一致性约束幻觉检测器（CCHD），将训练建模为约束优化问题。在原始文档-声明对的交叉熵基础上，补充：(i)释义一致性约束，限制跨释义视图的差异；(ii)标签保持约束，将释义与真实标签绑定。通过模型参数和每个视图的拉格朗日乘子的梯度下降-上升法求解，仅添加少量标量对偶变量，无推理时开销。

Result: 基于DeBERTa和Flan-T5骨干网络，CCHD在标准事实性基准测试上一致优于强基线方法（FactCG、MiniCheck和AlignScore），在幻觉检测方面表现出优越性。

Conclusion: CCHD通过利用语义等价释义的内在一致性，以更低的成本实现了更准确的幻觉检测，为大规模事实核查提供了有效的约束优化框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate factually inconsistent claims, motivating accurate and scalable hallucination detectors. Prior work largely enlarges training sets via synthesis or new annotations, introducing increasing cost and potential bias while underusing the consistency implied by semantically equivalent paraphrases. We propose Consistency-Constrained Hallucination Detector (CCHD), which formulates training as a constrained optimization problem. The standard cross-entropy on original document-claim pairs is complemented by (i) paraphrase-consistency constraints bounding divergence across paraphrased views, and (ii) label-preservation constraints tying paraphrases to ground truth. We solve the problem by gradient descent-ascent over model parameters and per-view Lagrange multipliers, adding only a few scalar dual variables and no inference-time overhead. With DeBERTa and Flan-T5 backbones, CCHD consistently outperforms strong baselines (FactCG, MiniCheck, and AlignScore) on standard factuality benchmarks, demonstrating its superiority on hallucination detection.

</details>


### [107] [TextEconomizer: Enhancing Lossy Text Compression with Denoising Transformers and Entropy Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08184)
*Mahbub E Sobhani,Anika Tasnim Rodela,Chowdhury Mofizur Rahman,Dewan Md. Farid,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TextEconomizer是一个创新的损失性文本压缩框架，结合了编码器-解码器结构和transformer神经网络，能够在保持语义质量的同时实现50-80%的压缩率，参数数量比现有模型少153倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管transformer模型在语言建模中占主导地位，但在序列到序列生成中整合上下文向量和熵编码仍然研究不足。关键挑战包括从编码器输出中识别最具信息量的上下文向量，以及通过熵编码提高存储效率，同时保证噪声文本条件下的高质量输出。

Method: 开发了TextEconomizer框架，采用编码器-解码器架构与transformer神经网络相结合。模型使用熵编码提升存储效率，无需先验数据集维度信息即可处理变长输入。同时评估了LSTM自编码器和LLaMAFormer改进型transformer。

Result: TextEconomizer实现了50-80%的压缩率，参数数量比可比模型少153倍，压缩比达5.39倍且不损失语义质量。LSTM自编码器达到67倍压缩比，参数少196倍；LLaMAFormer参数比ICAE少263倍且保持竞争性文本质量。

Conclusion: TextEconomizer在内存效率和高保真输出之间取得了显著平衡，超越了现有transformer模型，标志着损失性压缩技术在空间利用优化方面的突破，为文本摘要、自动化分析和数字档案提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Lossy text compression reduces data size while preserving core meaning, making it well-suited for summarization, automated analysis, and digital archives. Despite the dominance of transformer-based models in language modeling, integrating context vectors and entropy coding into Sequence-to-Sequence (Seq2Seq) generation remains underexplored. A key challenge lies in identifying the most informative context vectors from encoder output and incorporating entropy coding to enhance storage efficiency while maintaining high-quality outputs, even under noisy text. We introduce TextEconomizer, an encoder-decoder framework paired with a transformer neural network that reduces variable-sized inputs by 50% to 80% without prior knowledge of dataset dimensions. Our model achieves competitive compression ratios via entropy coding while delivering near-perfect text quality, assessed by BLEU, ROUGE, METEOR, and semantic similarity scores. TextEconomizer operates with approximately 153x fewer parameters than comparable models, achieving a 5.39x compression ratio without sacrificing semantic quality. We also evaluate an LSTM-based autoencoder achieving a state-of-the-art 67x compression ratio with 196x fewer parameters, and LLaMAFormer, a modified transformer with 263x fewer parameters than ICAE while maintaining competitive text quality. TextEconomizer significantly surpasses existing transformer-based models in balancing memory efficiency and high-fidelity outputs, marking a breakthrough in lossy compression with optimal space utilization.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [PathoSage: Towards Multi-Source Evidence Adjudication in Pathology via Experience-Aware Agentic Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07549)
*Chengyang Zhang,Wenchuan Zhang,Bo Li,Mengran Li,Bob Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathoSage是一个用于病理学多模态推理的三阶段框架，通过显式分离知识检索、证据收集和证据裁决来减少幻觉和上下文污染，特别关注工具可靠性建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在病理学中存在幻觉问题，而现有的智能体系统通常将工具输出和检索知识合并到共享上下文中，导致决策容易受到冲突证据和上下文污染的影响，特别是在patch-level推理方面存在挑战。

Method: 提出PathoSage三阶段框架：1) 知识检索；2) 证据收集；3) 证据裁决。核心组件是结构化证据审议，独立评估来自工具的异构证据，进行冲突分析，并在新上下文中生成最终判断以减少锚定偏差。还引入了无训练的Beta-Bernoulli经验系统，通过连续信用分配建模长期工具可靠性，并为未来工具使用构建相似性加权先验。

Result: 实验表明PathoSage有效缓解了VQA幻觉和分类器分歧，在病理学多模态推理任务中优于强基准的多模态大语言模型和智能体系统。

Conclusion: 显式证据裁决和可靠性感知的工具建模是构建稳健病理智能体的关键要素，PathoSage框架为解决patch-level病理学多模态推理中的挑战提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and agent workflows have shown strong promise for computational pathology, yet reliable patch-level reasoning remains challenging. End-to-end pathology MLLMs often hallucinate morphological features, while recent agentic systems usually merge tool outputs and retrieved knowledge into a shared context, making decisions vulnerable to conflicting evidence and context contamination. We propose PathoSage, a three-stage framework that explicitly separates knowledge retrieval, evidence collection, and evidence adjudication for patch-level pathology multimodal reasoning. Its core component, Structured Evidence Deliberation, independently evaluates heterogeneous evidence from tools, performs conflict analysis, and generates the final judgment in a fresh context to reduce anchoring bias. We further introduce a training-free Beta-Bernoulli experience system with continuous credit assignment to model long-term tool reliability and construct similarity-weighted priors for future tool use. Experiments show that PathoSage effectively mitigates VQA hallucinations and classifier disagreement, outperforming strong pathology MLLM and agentic baselines. Our results highlight explicit evidence adjudication and reliability-aware tool modeling as key ingredients for robust pathology agents.

</details>


### [109] [OmniMem: Perturbation-aware Memory Compression for Streaming Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07577)
*Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Yudong Yang,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMem是一个为音频-视觉大语言模型设计的内存高效流式处理框架，通过模态感知内存分配和扰动感知记忆选择，在保持长范围理解能力的同时显著减少KV缓存和视频token的内存消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视觉大语言模型在长视频理解方面具有强大潜力，但其推理过程受到视频token和KV缓存线性增长的根本限制，导致内存效率低下。

Method: OmniMem采用三种核心技术：1) 模态感知内存分配策略，分别管理视觉和音频上下文；2) 扰动感知内存选择，保留信息丰富且非冗余的KV状态；3) 预算感知微调，在现实部署约束下增强压缩效果。

Result: 在VideoMME Long、LVBench和LVOmniBench基准测试中，OmniMem相比强大的无训练压缩基线，在相同内存预算下绝对准确率提高了2-4%，微调后额外获得1-2%的增益。

Conclusion: OmniMem有效地解决了音频-视觉LLMs在处理长视频时的内存效率问题，通过针对性的压缩策略实现了更好的长范围理解能力，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (LLMs) hold strong promise for long-form video understanding, yet their long-video inference is fundamentally limited by the linear growth of video tokens and key-value (KV) caches. We present OmniMem, a memory-efficient streaming framework designed specifically for audio-visual LLMs. Unlike existing compression methods that treat all tokens uniformly, OmniMem introduces a modality-aware memory allocation strategy that separately manages visual and audio contexts, addressing the severe token imbalance between the two modalities. OmniMem further preserves informative and non-redundant KV states through perturbation-aware memory selection, enabling compact memory without sacrificing long-range understanding. To strengthen compression under realistic deployment constraints, we also explore budget-aware fine-tuning, which encourages the model to consolidate useful information into retained memory. Experiments on VideoMME Long, LVBench, and LVOmniBench with video-SALMONN 2+ and Qwen-2.5-Omni show that OmniMem consistently improves over strong training-free compression baselines by 2-4% absolute accuracy under the same memory budgets, with an additional 1-2% gain after fine-tuning.

</details>


### [110] [Syll: Open-Source Personal Automation with Cross-Surface Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07594)
*Bo Zhang,Borui Zhang,Chenghao Jiang,Minglei Shi,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Syll是一个开源、自托管的多模态代理框架，统一了API工具、CLI执行和可视化GUI控制，让AI代理能够协调跨异构界面的计算机使用，同时简化用户与代理之间的信息交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个人AI代理系统大多局限于单一接口，缺乏对用户教学和可审计性的支持。随着AI代理需要在API、shell、网页界面和桌面GUI等多种界面上操作，需要一个统一的框架来协调这些异构接口，并支持用户教学和检查。

Method: 开发了Syll多模态代理框架，其核心是双向用户-代理交互层：用户通过直接演示教授程序，Syll将其编译成可重用技能；代理执行结果转换为多模态证据（日志、关键帧、审批检查点）供检查和控制。框架将内存、技能、例程和治理外部化为可编辑的本地工件。

Result: 在Adobe Photoshop、Adobe Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder等生产桌面应用程序上验证了实现。通过面向机制的研究验证了多模态路由、可教学的GUI重放和持久化本地工件等功能。

Conclusion: Syll可以作为个人自动化实践的开放源代码基础，用户可以教学、检查并持续扩展。它解决了跨异构接口协调和用户-代理交互的双向性问题，提供了可审计、可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Personal AI agents must increasingly operate across APIs, shells, web surfaces, and desktop GUIs, yet many systems remain tuned to a single interface and offer limited support for user teaching and auditability. We present Syll, an open-source, self-hosted multimodal agent harness that unifies MCP/API tools, CLI execution, and visual GUI control in a modular runtime, enabling agents to coordinate computer use across heterogeneous interfaces while streamlining how users and agents exchange information. At the core of Syll is a bidirectional user-agent interaction layer: users teach procedures through direct demonstration, which Syll compiles into reusable skills; agent execution is translated back into multimodal evidence -- logs, keyframes, and approval checkpoints -- for inspection and control. Syll further externalizes memory, skills, routines, and governance as editable local artifacts, supporting straightforward inspection, extension, and downstream development. Our implementation has been validated on production desktop applications including Adobe Photoshop, Adobe Audition, Stardew Valley, macOS Finder and others. We report mechanism-oriented studies that validate multimodal routing, teachable GUI replay, and persistent local artifacts. We hope Syll can serve as a practical open-source foundation for personal automation that users can teach, inspect, and continuously extend.

</details>


### [111] [A case study of evaluating AI agents on a neuroscience data-to-discovery pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07718)
*Kai A. Horstmann,Ethan Lin,Alice A. Robie,Jennifer J. Sun,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文实证研究了通用编码智能体在果蝇光遗传学数据到发现管道中的应用，发现智能体可以解决部分管道阶段任务，但在需要科学判断、端到端管道整合和大规模数据泛化方面仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理AI工具在自动化科学研发管道中具有潜力，特别是那些需要领域专家花费数天到数月构建的阶段。科学家们关心的是正确性和鲁棒性，而不是实现细节，因此探索编码智能体在实际科学管道中的应用价值具有重要意义。

Method: 采用实证研究方法，在果蝇光遗传学数据到发现管道上评估通用编码智能体。研究设置了比现有基准更大的任务规模、数量级更大的数据集，并采用基于领域专家标准的评估标准。分析智能体的代码迭代过程，特别关注其在缺乏预定义评估标准时的表现。

Result: 智能体能够解决管道中的几个单独阶段，表明阶段级自动化是可行的。然而，当没有预定义标准可迭代时（需要科学判断来评估当前解决方案），智能体面临最大困难。智能体尝试通过视觉检查中间输出进行自我评估，但大多无法正确解释或采取适当行动。正确解决端到端管道需要在所有阶段连续成功，这超出了智能体当前能力。研究还发现了现有基准中缺少的挑战：计算资源管理和大规模数据集的泛化问题。

Conclusion: 智能体在科学管道自动化中具有潜力，但在需要科学判断、端到端整合和大规模数据泛化方面仍需突破。研究提炼了构建科学任务和严格评估开放问题的原则，为未来智能体开发提供了方向。科学智能体的成功不仅需要编码能力，还需要领域知识和科学推理能力。

Abstract: Agentic AI tools offer a promising path to automating software development bottlenecks in scientific research pipelines, particularly for stages that take domain experts days to months to build, where scientists care about correctness and robustness, not implementation details. We present an empirical study of general-purpose coding agents on a fly optogenetics data-to-discovery pipeline. We assess agents on tasks substantially larger than existing benchmarks, datasets orders of magnitude bigger, and evaluation criteria grounded in domain expert standards. We show that agents can solve several individual pipeline stages, suggesting stage-level automation is tractable. By analyzing agents' code iterations, we show that they struggle most when there is not a pre-defined criterion to iterate on, and they must instead use their scientific judgment to assess their current solution, a key open challenge.
